local function khSnapshotAndSave(ai)
if ae and ae._ApplyingConfig then return true,nil end
khSnapshotRuntimeConfig()
return khSaveConfigNow(ai or"runtime-change")

local function GetPlayerData()
if not x then return nil end
return x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
end

local function GetModule(ai,aj)
local ak=ai:FindFirstChild(aj)
if ak then
local al,am=pcall(require,ak)
if al and type(am)=="table"then return am end
end
return nil
end

local function GetAvailableBaits()
local ai=GetPlayerData()
if not ai then return{}end
local aj={}
local ak=ai:FindFirstChild"Bait"
if not ak then return{}end
for al,am in pairs(ak:GetChildren())do
if am:IsA"NumberValue"and am.Value>0 then
table.insert(aj,am.Name)
end
end
return aj
end

local function GetBaitStats(ai)
local aj=GetModule(w.Bait,ai)
if aj then return{Luck=aj.Luck or 0}end
return{Luck=0}
end

local function GetBestBait()
local ai=GetAvailableBaits()
local aj,ak=(-1)
for al,am in ipairs(ai)do
local V=GetBaitStats(am)
if V.Luck>aj then
aj=V.Luck;ak=am
end
end
return ak
end

local function EquipBait(ai)
if not ai then return false end
return pcall(function()safeInvoke("EquipBait",ai)end)
end

local function GetOwnedRods()
local ai=GetPlayerData()
if not ai then return{}end
local aj={}
for ak,al in pairs(ai.FishingRodInventory:GetChildren())do
if al:IsA"Folder"and al.Owned and al.Owned.Value==true then
table.insert(aj,al.Name)
end
end
return aj
end

local function GetRodStats(ai)
local aj=GetModule(w.Inventory,ai)
if aj then return{Power=aj.Power or 0,Luck=aj.Luck or 0}end
return{Power=0,Luck=0}
end

local function GetBestRod()
local ai=GetOwnedRods()
local aj,ak=(-1)
for al,am in ipairs(ai)do
local V=GetRodStats(am)
local W=V.Power+V.Luck
if W>aj then
aj=W;ak=am
end
end
return ak
end

local function EquipRod(ai)
if not ai then return false end
return pcall(function()safeInvoke("EquipFishingRod",ai)end)
end

local function GetAvailableOrbs()
local ai=GetPlayerData()
if not ai then return{}end
local aj={}
local ak=ai:FindFirstChild"Orb"
if not ak then return{}end
for al,am in pairs(ak:GetChildren())do
if am:IsA"Folder"then
local V=am:FindFirstChild"Equipping"
if V and V.Value==false then
table.insert(aj,am.Name)
end
end
end
return aj
end

local function GetOrbStats(ai)
local aj=GetPlayerData()
if not aj then return{}end
local ak=aj.Orb:FindFirstChild(ai)
if not ak then return{}end
local al={}
for am,V in pairs(ak:GetChildren())do
if V:IsA"NumberValue"and V.Name~="Equipping"and V.Name~="Favorite"then
al[V.Name]=V.Value
end
end
return al
end

local function GetBestOrb()
local ai=GetAvailableOrbs()
local aj,ak=(-1)
for al,am in ipairs(ai)do
local V=GetOrbStats(am)
local W=(V.Damage or 0)*2+(V.Luck or 0)*1.5+(V.Cash or 0)*0.5
if W>aj then
aj=W;ak=am
end
end
return ak
end

local function EquipOrb(ai)
if not ai then return false end
return pcall(function()safeInvoke("EquipOrb",ai)end)
end

local function GetBuyableBaits()
local ai={}
if not w or not w:FindFirstChild"Bait"then return{"No bait available"}end
for aj,ak in pairs(w.Bait:GetChildren())do
if ak:IsA"ModuleScript"then
local al,am=pcall(require,ak)
if al and type(am)=="table"and am.Price and am.Price>0 then
table.insert(ai,ak.Name)
end
end
end
if#ai==0 then return{"No bait available"}end
return ai
end

local function saveRoutineFlags()
I.savedRoutineFlags={
AutoCast=L.Flags.AutoCast,
AnchorLock=L.Flags.AnchorLock,
AutoSkill=L.Flags.AutoSkill,
AutoSell=M.AutoSell,
AutoSkillCheck=L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck,
AntiAFK=L.Flags.AntiAFK,
AutoFarmBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss,
AutoFarmSecretBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss,
AutoBuy=M.AutoBuy,
AutoEquipBait=M.AutoEquipBait,
AutoEquipRod=M.AutoEquipRod,
}
end

local function restoreRoutineFlags()
if I.savedRoutineFlags then
L.Flags.AutoCast=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoCast
L.Flags.AnchorLock=I.savedRoutineFlags.AnchorLock
L.Flags.AutoSkill=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoSkill
M.AutoSell=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoSell
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoSkillCheck
L.Flags.AntiAFK=I.savedRoutineFlags.AntiAFK
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoFarmBoss
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoFarmSecretBoss
M.AutoBuy=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoBuy
M.AutoEquipBait=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoEquipBait
M.AutoEquipRod=I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoEquipRod
I.savedRoutineFlags=nil
else
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
end
end

local function isRodTool(ai)
if not ai or not ai:IsA"Tool"then return false end
local aj=tostring(ai:GetAttribute"Type"or""):lower()
local ak=ai.Name:lower()
if aj:find("fishing rod",1,true)or aj=="fishingrod"then return true end
if ak:find("rod",1,true)or ak:find("cần",1,true)then return true end
return false
end

local function isCurrentlyHoldingRod()
local ai=s.Character
if not ai then return false end

local aj=ai:GetAttribute"Type"
if aj and string.lower(tostring(aj)):find("fishing rod",1,true)then return true end

for ak,al in ipairs(ai:GetChildren())do
if isRodTool(al)then return true end
end
return false
end

local function ensureRodEquipped()
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()then return true end

local ai=s.Character
local aj=ai and ai:FindFirstChildOfClass"Humanoid"
local ak=s:FindFirstChildOfClass"Backpack"


if aj and ak then
for al,am in ipairs(ak:GetChildren())do
if isRodTool(am)then
pcall(function()aj:EquipTool(am)end)
task.wait(0.2)
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()then return true end
end
end
end


local al=GetBestRod()
if al then
pcall(function()EquipRod(al)end)
task.wait(0.35)

ak=s:FindFirstChildOfClass"Backpack"
aj=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass"Humanoid"
if aj and ak then
for am,V in ipairs(ak:GetChildren())do
if isRodTool(V)then
pcall(function()aj:EquipTool(V)end)
task.wait(0.2)
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()then return true end
end
end
end
end


local am=t and t:FindFirstChild"ToggleHotbar"
if am then
pcall(function()
if am:IsA"RemoteFunction"then
am:InvokeServer("1",nil)
elseif am:IsA"RemoteEvent"then
am:FireServer("1",nil)
end
end)
task.wait(0.25)
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()then return true end

pcall(function()
if am:IsA"RemoteFunction"then
am:InvokeServer(1,nil)
end
end)
task.wait(0.2)
end

return isCurrentlyHoldingRod()
end


local function forceEquipRodOnce()
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()then return true end
pcall(ensureRodEquipped)
return isCurrentlyHoldingRod()
end

local function enableBasicFishing(ai)
if not I.savedRoutineFlags then saveRoutineFlags()end
L.Flags.MainFarm=true
L.Flags.AutoCast=true
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
if ai==true then
L.Flags.AutoSkill=true
end
L.Flags.AntiAFK=true
M.AutoEquipRod=true

task.defer(function()
pcall(forceEquipRodOnce)
end)
end

local function disableAllFishing()
restoreRoutineFlags()
end

local function getTotalPower()
local ai=0
local aj=s.Character
if aj then
local ak=aj:FindFirstChild"Stats"
if ak then
local al=ak:FindFirstChild"RodPower"
if al and al:IsA"NumberValue"then ai=ai+al.Value end
local am=ak:FindFirstChild"FishPower"
if am and am:IsA"NumberValue"then ai=ai+am.Value end
end
local al=aj:GetAttribute"RodPower"
if al and type(al)=="number"and al>0 and ai==0 then ai=al end
end
if ai==0 then
local ak=s.UserId
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(ak))
if al then
local am=al:FindFirstChild"FishingRod"
if am then
local V=am.Value
if V and V~=""then
local W=w:FindFirstChild"Inventory"and w.Inventory:FindFirstChild(V)
if W then
local X,Y=pcall(require,W)
if X and type(Y)=="table"then
ai=Y.Power or 0
end
end
end
end
end
end
return ai
end

local function getRecommendedIsland()
local ai=getTotalPower()
local aj={}
for ak,al in ipairs(I.islands)do
if(al.power or 0)<=ai then
table.insert(aj,al)
end
end
table.sort(aj,function(ak,al)return ak.power>al.power end)
return aj[2]or aj[1]
end

local function isHoldingFishingRod()
local ai=s.Character
if not ai then return false end
local aj=ai:GetAttribute"Type"
if aj and string.lower(tostring(aj))=="fishing rod"then return true end
return false
end

local function isEnzoInFight()
local am=false
pcall(function()
local V=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
local W=V and V:FindFirstChild"Boss"
local X=W and W:FindFirstChild"Enzo"
am=X~=nil and X:GetAttribute"InFight"==true
end)
if am then return true end
local V=v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
local W=V and V:FindFirstChild"BossFightBar"
return W~=nil and W.Visible==true
end

local function setAutoEnzo(am)
am=am==true
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=am
if L.BossFightThread then
task.cancel(L.BossFightThread);L.BossFightThread=nil
end
if a.KhfreshBossFightThread then
task.cancel(a.KhfreshBossFightThread);a.KhfreshBossFightThread=nil
end
if am and u then
L.BossFightThread=task.spawn(function()
while L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck do
if isEnzoInFight()then break end
pcall(function()u:FireServer("Enzo",L.Flags.BossFightMode or"Normal")end)

task.wait(0.25+math.random()*0.05)
end
L.BossFightThread=nil
a.KhfreshBossFightThread=nil
end)
a.KhfreshBossFightThread=L.BossFightThread
end
end

ak:CreateToggle{Name="Auto Enzo",Icon="zap",Default=false,Callback=function(am)setAutoEnzo(am==true)end}
ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Auto Enzo Fight Mode",
Options={"Normal","Hard","Nightmare"},
Default="Normal",
Callback=function(am)
L.Flags.BossFightMode=am or"Normal"
if L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck then setAutoEnzo(true)end
end
}

do
local ao="Nameless Octoparasite"
local ap={"A","S","D"}
local aq={A=Enum.KeyCode.A,S=Enum.KeyCode.S,D=Enum.KeyCode.D}
local ar=0.22
local _=0.8214285714285714
local as=0.85

I.Rhythm={
Enabled=false,
Mode="Risk",
RiskRate=25,
Band=0.17,
Lag=0.03,
Active=false,
SignalUntil=0,
Config=nil,
Method=nil,
RiskThread=nil,
NextPress={},
Stats={hit=0,spam=0},
}
local at=I.Rhythm

local function rhyFindNameless()
local au={}
local av=q:FindFirstChild"Ocean"
if av then
local aw=av:FindFirstChild"Boss"
if aw then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"FishName"
if ax then table.insert(au,ax)end
table.insert(au,aw)
end
table.insert(au,av)
end
local aw=q:FindFirstChild"Fishes"
if aw then table.insert(au,aw)end
for ax,ay in ipairs(au)do
local az,aA=pcall(function()return ay:FindFirstChild(ao,true)end)
if az and aA then return aA end
end
return nil
end

local function rhyPivotOf(au)
if not au then return nil end
local av,aw=pcall(function()
if au:IsA"BasePart"then return au.CFrame end
if au:IsA"Model"then
if au.PrimaryPart then return au.PrimaryPart.CFrame end
return au:GetPivot()
end
local av=au:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
if av then return av.CFrame end
return nil
end)
if av and aw then return aw end
return nil
end

local function rhyTeleportToNameless()
local au=rhyFindNameless()
if not au then
ae:Notify{Title="Nameless",Message="Cannot find "..
ao.." in Workspace (boss not spawned yet?)",Duration=3}
return false
end
local av=rhyPivotOf(au)
if not av then
ae:Notify{Title="Nameless",Message="Found boss but could not get its position!",Duration=3}
return false
end
local aw=s.Character
local ax=aw and(aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"or aw.PrimaryPart)
if not ax then
ae:Notify{Title="Nameless",Message="Character not found!",Duration=2}
return false
end
ax.CFrame=av+Vector3.new(0,12,0)
ae:Notify{Title="Nameless",Message="Teleported to "..ao,Duration=2}
return true
end

local function rhyPress(au,av)
local aw={
function()
if not firesignal then return false end
local aw=au and au:FindFirstChild"Button"
if not aw then return false end
if not(aw:IsA"TextButton"or aw:IsA"ImageButton")then return false end
firesignal(aw.MouseButton1Click)
return true
end,
function()
local aw=game:GetService"VirtualInputManager"
aw:SendKeyEvent(true,aq[av],false,game)
aw:SendKeyEvent(false,aq[av],false,game)
return true
end,
function()
local aw=game:GetService"VirtualInputManager"
aw:SendKeyEvent(true,aq[av],false,nil)
aw:SendKeyEvent(false,aq[av],false,nil)
return true
end,
}
if at.Method then
local ax,ay=pcall(aw[at.Method])
if ax and ay then return true end
at.Method=nil
end
for ax,ay in ipairs(aw)do
local az,aA=pcall(ay)
if az and aA then
at.Method=ax
return true
end
end
return false
end

local function rhyGui()
local au=v and v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
if not au then return nil,nil end
return au:FindFirstChild"Rhythm",au
end

local function rhyUiActive()
local au,av=rhyGui()
if not au or not av then return false end
return au.Visible==true and av.Visible==true
end

local function rhyMaxBand()
local au=ar-_*(at.Lag or 0)-0.02
if au<0.01 then au=0.01 end
return au
end

local function rhyScan(au)
local av=rhyGui()
if not av then return end
for aw,ax in ipairs(ap)do
local ay=av:FindFirstChild("Progression"..ax)
if ay then
local az=as
local aA=ay:FindFirstChild"BarFrame"
if aA then
local aB,aC=pcall(function()return aA.Position.Y.Scale end)
if aB and type(aC)=="number"then az=aC end
end
local aB=clamp(at.Band,0.01,rhyMaxBand())
local aC=0
for aD,aE in ipairs(ay:GetChildren())do
if aE.Name=="Note_FX"then
local aF,aG=pcall(function()return aE.Position.Y.Scale end)
if aF and type(aG)=="number"then
if math.abs((aG+_*at.Lag)-az)<=aB then
aC=aC+1
end
end
end
end
if aC>5 then aC=5 end
for aD=1,aC do
if rhyPress(ay,ax)then
at.Stats.hit=at.Stats.hit+1
end
end
end
end
end

local function rhyStartRisk()
if at.RiskThread then return end
at.RiskThread=task.spawn(function()
while alive()and at.Enabled and at.Mode=="Risk"and at.Active do
local au=clamp(tonumber(at.RiskRate)or 25,1,200)
local av=math.max(1,math.floor(au/20))
for aw=1,av do
pcall(function()safeFire("RhythmHit","hit")end)
at.Stats.spam=at.Stats.spam+1
end
task.wait(av/au)
end
at.RiskThread=nil
end)
end

if a.KhfreshRhyStart then pcall(function()a.KhfreshRhyStart:Disconnect()end)end
if a.KhfreshRhyStop then pcall(function()a.KhfreshRhyStop:Disconnect()end)end
if a.KhfreshRhyLoop then pcall(function()a.KhfreshRhyLoop:Disconnect()end)end

pcall(function()
local au=t:FindFirstChild"RhythmStart"
if au then
a.KhfreshRhyStart=au.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(av)
if not alive()then return end
at.Config=av
at.Active=true
at.SignalUntil=tick()+8
at.Stats={hit=0,spam=0}
at.NextPress={}
if at.Enabled then
local aw=av and(av.FinalMaxHP or av.HPFinalPhase)
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Nameless",
Message="Rhythm started ["..
tostring(at.Mode).."]"..(aw and(" | HP "..tostring(aw))or""),
Duration=3,
}
if at.Mode=="Risk"then rhyStartRisk()end
end
end)
end
local av=t:FindFirstChild"RhythmStop"
if av then
a.KhfreshRhyStop=av.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
at.Active=false
at.SignalUntil=0
end)
end
end)

a.KhfreshRhyLoop=l.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not alive()or not at.Enabled then return end
local au=tick()
at.Active=rhyUiActive()or au<(at.SignalUntil or 0)
if not at.Active then return end
if at.Mode=="Risk"then rhyStartRisk()end
rhyScan(au)
end)

local au=aj:CreateSection"Auto Nameless Octoparasite"
au:CreateButton{Name="Teleport to Nameless Octoparasite",Callback=function()pcall(
rhyTeleportToNameless)end}
au:CreateDropdown{
Name="Nameless Mode",
Options={"Safe","Risk"},
Default="Safe",
Callback=function(av)
at.Mode=av
if av=="Safe"then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Nameless",Message=
"Safe: wait until the note reaches the line before pressing (never miss)",Duration=3}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Nameless",Message=
"Risk: fire the RhythmHit remote directly -> fast but easier to detect",Duration=4}
if at.Enabled and at.Active then rhyStartRisk()end
end
end
}
au:CreateTextbox{
Name="Risk hits/sec (1-200)",
Placeholder="25",
Default="25",
Callback=function(av)
local aw=tonumber(av)
if aw then
at.RiskRate=clamp(math.floor(aw),1,200)
else
at.RiskRate=25
end
end
}
au:CreateTextbox{
Name="Safe band (0.01-0.17)",
Placeholder="0.17",
Default="0.17",
Callback=function(av)
local aw=tonumber(av)
if aw then
at.Band=clamp(aw,0.01,rhyMaxBand())
else
at.Band=0.17
end
end
}
au:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Nameless",
Default=false,
Callback=function(av)
at.Enabled=av
if av then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Nameless",Message="Enabled ["..
tostring(at.Mode).."]. Waiting for RhythmStart from server.",Duration=3}
if at.Active and at.Mode=="Risk"then rhyStartRisk()end
else
at.Active=false
at.SignalUntil=0
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Nameless",Message="Disabled | hit "..
tostring(at.Stats.hit).." | remote "..tostring(at.Stats.spam),Duration=3}
end
end
}
end

I.favorites.autoEnabled=as

if I.favorites.thread then

else
if I.favorites.thread then

I.favorites.thread=nil

end
end
}

do
local ao=aj:CreateSection"Auto Pray Spirit"
local ap=false
local aq
local ar=0
local as=2

local function getSpiritRoot(at,au)
local av=at:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart",true)
if av and av:IsA"BasePart"then return av end
if at.PrimaryPart then return at.PrimaryPart end
local aw=at:FindFirstChild("Talk",true)
if aw and aw:IsA"BasePart"then return aw end
local ax=au and au.Parent
if ax and ax:IsA"BasePart"then return ax end
return nil
end

local function findSpiritPrompt(at)

for au,av in ipairs(at:GetDescendants())do
if av:IsA"Model"then
local aw=av:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart",true)
local ax=aw and aw:FindFirstChild"Talk"
local ay=ax and ax:FindFirstChild"ProximityPrompt"
if ay and ay:IsA"ProximityPrompt"and ay.Enabled then
return av,ay,aw
end
end
end

local au=at:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt",true)
if au and au.Enabled then
local av=au:FindFirstAncestorOfClass"Model"or at
return av,au,getSpiritRoot(av,au)
end
return nil,nil,nil
end

local function fireSpiritPrompt(at)
if not at then return false end
pcall(function()
at.RequiresLineOfSight=false
at.HoldDuration=0
at.MaxActivationDistance=math.max(at.MaxActivationDistance,25)
end)

if type(fireproximityprompt)=="function"then
local au=pcall(function()fireproximityprompt(at)end)
if au then return true end
end

if type(firesignal)=="function"then
local au=pcall(function()
if at.PromptButtonHoldBegan then firesignal(at.PromptButtonHoldBegan)end
task.wait(0.08)
if at.PromptButtonHoldEnded then firesignal(at.PromptButtonHoldEnded)end
end)
if au then return true end
end

return false
end

local function scanAndPray()
local at=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
local au=at and at:FindFirstChild"God"
if not au then return false,"God folder not found"end
if tick()-ar<as then return false,nil end

local av,aw,ax=findSpiritPrompt(au)
if not aw or not ax then return false,"Spirit prompt not found"end

local ay=s.Character
local az=ay and ay:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not az then return false,"Character not ready"end


az.CFrame=CFrame.new(ax.Position+Vector3.new(0,2,0))
task.wait(0.12)

local aA=fireSpiritPrompt(aw)
if aA then
ar=tick()
return true,"Prayed to "..tostring(av and av.Name or"Spirit")
end
return false,"Could not trigger spirit prompt"
end

local function startPray()
if ap then return end
ap=true
aq=task.spawn(function()
while ap and alive()do
local at,au=scanAndPray()
if at then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Pray",Message=au,Duration=2}
end
task.wait(1.0)
end
end)
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Pray",Message="Enabled",Duration=2}
end

local function stopPray()
ap=false
if aq then
pcall(function()task.cancel(aq)end)
aq=nil
end
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Pray",Message="Disabled",Duration=2}
end

ao:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Teleport + Interact (Pray) Spirit",
Icon="hand-heart",
Default=false,
Callback=function(at)
if at==true then startPray()else stopPray()end
end
}
end

do












local ao=aj:CreateSection{Title="Auto Farm Selected Fish",Icon="lock",Locked=true}
local ap={}
local aq={}
local ar={}
local as={}
local at=1
local au=false
local av
local aw

local ax={
["Island 1"]=1,["Island 2"]=2,["Island 3"]=3,["Island 4"]=4,
["Island 5"]=5,["Island 6"]=6,["Island 7"]=7,["Island 8"]=8,
["Island 9"]=9,["Island 10"]=10,["Island 11"]=11,
["Beginning Isle"]=1,["Bamboo Isle"]=2,["Fallout Isle"]=3,
["Sovereign Isle"]=4,["Perch Isle"]=5,["Frost Isle"]=6,
["Coconut Isle"]=7,["Amber Isle"]=8,["Battlefield Isle"]=9,
["Mistpeak Isle"]=10,
}

local ay={}
local az={}

I.SelectedAutoFarmCastToken=nil
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmActiveCastSerial=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex=1
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslands={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastIssuedAt=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmLastCancelAt=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland=nil
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishNameAtCast=""

local function islandOf(aA)
local aB=tostring(aA or"")
local aC=ax[aB]
if aC then return aC end
aC=tonumber(aB:match"(%d+)")
if aC and aC>=1 and aC<=11 then return aC end
return nil
end

local function pureFishName(aA)
return normalizeSelectedFishName(aA)
end

local function buildFishEntries()
table.clear(ay)
table.clear(az)
pcall(function()
local aA=w and w:FindFirstChild"FishingAreaRarity"
local aB=aA and require(aA)
if type(aB)~="table"then return end
for aC,aD in pairs(aB)do
local aE=islandOf(aC)
if aE and type(aD)=="table"then
for aF in pairs(aD)do
if type(aF)=="string"and aF~=""then
local aG=aF.." | Isle "..tostring(aE)
local _={Name=aF,Island=aE,Label=aG}
ay[#ay+1]=_
az[aG]=_
end
end
end
end
end)
table.sort(ay,function(aA,aB)
if aA.Island~=aB.Island then return aA.Island>aB.Island end
return string.lower(aA.Name)<string.lower(aB.Name)
end)
local aA={}
for aB,aC in ipairs(ay)do aA[#aA+1]=aC.Label end
if#aA==0 then aA={"No fish data (rejoin)"}end
return aA
end

local aA=buildFishEntries()

local function clearCaughtState()
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland={}
for aB in pairs(ar)do
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland[aB]={}
end
end

local function rebuildSelectedStateFromEntries(aB)
ap={}
aq={}
ar={}
as={}
local aC,aD={},{}

for aE,aF in ipairs(aB or{})do
if type(aF)=="table"and type(aF.Name)=="string"and aF.Name~=""and aF.Island then
local aG=tostring(aF.Label or(aF.Name.." | Isle "..tostring(aF.Island)))
if not aC[aG]then
aC[aG]=true
ap[#ap+1]=aF
aq[aG]=true
local _=normalizeSelectedFishName(aF.Name)
if _~=""then
ar[aF.Island]=ar[aF.Island]or{}
ar[aF.Island][_]=true
if not aD[aF.Island]then
aD[aF.Island]=true
as[#as+1]=aF.Island
end
end
end
end
end

table.sort(as,function(aE,aF)return aE>aF end)
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslands={}
for aE,aF in ipairs(as)do
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslands[aE]=aF
end
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland={}
for aE,aF in pairs(ar)do
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland[aE]={}
for aG in pairs(aF)do
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland[aE][aG]=true
end
end
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishSet={}
for aE,aF in ipairs(ap)do
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishSet[aF.Name]=true
end
clearCaughtState()
end

local function valuesToEntries(aB)
local aC,aD={},{}
if type(aB)~="table"then return aC end
local function push(aE)
if type(aE)~="string"or aE==""or aE=="No fish data (rejoin)"then return end
local aF=az[aE]
if not aF then
local aG=pureFishName(aE)
for _,aH in ipairs(ay)do
if pureFishName(aH.Name)==aG then
aF=aH
break
end
end
end
if aF and not aD[aF.Label]then
aD[aF.Label]=true
aC[#aC+1]=aF
end
end
for aE=1,#aB do push(aB[aE])end
if#aC==0 then
for aE,aF in pairs(aB)do
if aF==true then push(aE)
elseif type(aF)=="string"then push(aF)end
end
end
return aC
end

local aB={}
do
local aC=I.SelectedAutoFarmFishEntries
if type(aC)=="table"then
for aD,aE in ipairs(aC)do
if type(aE)=="table"and aE.Label then
aB[#aB+1]=aE.Label
end
end
end
if#aB==0 then
local aD=I.selectedAutoFarmFish
if type(aD)=="table"then
local aE={}
for aF,aG in ipairs(aD)do
if type(aG)=="string"then aE[normalizeSelectedFishName(aG)]=true end
end
for aF,aG in ipairs(ay)do
if aE[normalizeSelectedFishName(aG.Name)]then
aB[#aB+1]=aG.Label
end
end
end
end
end

local aC=ao:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select Fish (Multi)",Multi=true,Options=aA,Default=aB,
Callback=function(aC)
rebuildSelectedStateFromEntries(valuesToEntries(aC))
I.selectedAutoFarmFish={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishEntries={}
for aD,aE in ipairs(ap)do
I.selectedAutoFarmFish[#I.selectedAutoFarmFish+1]=aE.Name
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishEntries[#I.SelectedAutoFarmFishEntries+1]={
Name=aE.Name,Island=aE.Island,Label=aE.Label
}
end
pcall(function()khSnapshotAndSave"selected fish changed"end)
ae:Notify{
Title="Selected Fish",
Message="Selected "..tostring(#ap).." fish entries across "..tostring(#as).." island(s)",
Duration=2,
}
end,
}
if#aB>0 then rebuildSelectedStateFromEntries(valuesToEntries(aB))end

local function getIslandEntry(aD)
aD=tonumber(aD)
if not aD then return nil end


local aE=I.islands and I.islands[aD]
if aE and aE.cframe then return aE end
local aF="Island "..tostring(aD)
for aG,aH in ipairs(I.islands or{})do
if aH.name==aF then return aH end
end
return nil
end

local function teleportToSelectedIsland(aD,aE)
local aF=getIslandEntry(aD)
if not aF or not aF.cframe then return false end
local aG=s.Character
local aH=aG and aG:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not aH then return false end
local _=aF.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
local aI=(aH.Position-_).Magnitude
if aI<=20 and not aE then return true end
if aG:GetAttribute"Fishing"or aG:GetAttribute"Retractable"then return false end
aH.CFrame=CFrame.new(_)
task.wait()
local aJ=(aH.Position-_).Magnitude
return aJ<=35
end

local function waitForFishingIdle(aD)
local aE=tick()+(aD or 2)
while tick()<aE and alive()do
local aF=s.Character
if not aF then return false end
if not aF:GetAttribute"Fishing"and not aF:GetAttribute"Retractable"then
return true
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
return false
end

local function cancelExistingCastBeforeStart()
local aD=s.Character
if not aD then return end
if not aD:GetAttribute"Fishing"and not aD:GetAttribute"Retractable"then return end
local aE=aD:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if aE then
pcall(function()safeFire("Fishing",aE.CFrame)end)
end
waitForFishingIdle(2)
end

local function selectedFishEquipRod()
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()or isHoldingFishingRod()then return true end
pcall(forceEquipRodOnce)
if isCurrentlyHoldingRod()or isHoldingFishingRod()then return true end
task.wait(0.15)
return isCurrentlyHoldingRod()or isHoldingFishingRod()
end

local function selectedCurrentIsland()
if#as==0 then return nil end
local aD=tonumber(at)or 1
if aD<1 or aD>#as then aD=1 end
at=aD
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex=aD
return as[aD]
end

























local function selectedFishAutoCast()
if not au or I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning~=true then return false end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase~="Idle"then return false end
local aD=s.Character
if not aD then return false end
if aD:GetAttribute"Fishing"or aD:GetAttribute"Retractable"then return false end
local aE=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland)or selectedCurrentIsland()
if not aE then return false end

local aF=getIslandEntry(aE)
local aG=aD:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not aF or not aG then return false end
local aH=aF.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
if(aG.Position-aH).Magnitude>35 then return false end
if not selectedFishEquipRod()then return false end

I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial=(tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)or 0)+1
local aI=I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial
I.SelectedAutoFarmActiveCastSerial=aI
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastToken=nil
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastIssuedAt=tick()
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="WaitingForMinigame"
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishNameAtCast=readProgressFishName()


pcall(function()safeFire("Fishing",aG.CFrame)end)
warn("[Khfresh][SelectedFish] CAST serial="..tostring(aI).." island="..tostring(aE))
return true
end

local function startSelectedFishFarm()
local aD
pcall(function()
if aC and type(aC.Get)=="function"then aD=aC.Get()end
end)
if aD then
local aE=valuesToEntries(aD)
if#aE>0 then rebuildSelectedStateFromEntries(aE)end
end
if#ap==0 and#aB>0 then rebuildSelectedStateFromEntries(valuesToEntries(aB))end
if#ap==0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Selected Fish",Message="Select at least one fish first.",Duration=3}
return false
end

au=true
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning=true
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmActiveCastSerial=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastToken=nil
at=1
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex=1
aw=nil
clearCaughtState()
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland=selectedCurrentIsland()
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastIssuedAt=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmLastCancelAt=0



L.Flags.MainFarm=false
L.Flags.AutoCast=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
L.Flags.AnchorLock=false
M.AutoEquipRod=true
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=false
L.BossFarm.HasHooked=false
L.BossFarm.CurrentToken=nil

cancelExistingCastBeforeStart()
waitForFishingIdle(1.5)

local aE=selectedCurrentIsland()
if aE then
local aF=teleportToSelectedIsland(aE,true)
warn("[Khfresh][SelectedFish] START island="..tostring(aE).." teleport="..tostring(aF))
if aF then
task.wait(0.25)
selectedFishEquipRod()
aw=aE
end
end
return true
end

local function stopSelectedFishFarm()
au=false
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning=false
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishSet={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland={}
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastToken=nil
I.SelectedAutoFarmActiveCastSerial=0
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial=(tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)or 0)+1
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland=nil
I.SelectedAutoFarmCastIssuedAt=0
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=false
L.BossFarm.HasHooked=false
L.BossFarm.CurrentToken=nil
L.Flags.MainFarm=false
L.Flags.AutoCast=false
M.AutoEquipRod=false
if av then pcall(task.cancel,av)av=nil end
end

ao:CreateToggle{
Name="Enable Auto Farm Selected Fish",Default=false,
Callback=function(aD)
aD=aD==true
if not aD then
stopSelectedFishFarm()
ae:Notify{Title="Selected Fish",Message="Stopped.",Duration=2}
return
end
if not startSelectedFishFarm()then return end
if av then pcall(task.cancel,av)end
av=task.spawn(function()
while au and alive()do
pcall(function()
if#ap==0 then stopSelectedFishFarm();return end
local aE=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland)or selectedCurrentIsland()
if not aE then return end

local aF=I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase or"Idle"
local aG=s.Character
if not aG then return end

if aF=="Idle"then
local aH=getIslandEntry(aE)
local aI=aG:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not aH or not aI then return end
local aJ=aH.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
local _=(aI.Position-aJ).Magnitude
if _>35 then
local aK=teleportToSelectedIsland(aE,false)
if aK then
aw=aE
task.wait(0.2)
selectedFishEquipRod()
end
return
end
if aw~=aE then
aw=aE
selectedFishEquipRod()
end
selectedFishAutoCast()
return
end

if aF=="WaitingForMinigame"then
local aH=tick()-(tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastIssuedAt)or tick())
if aH>=7 and not aG:GetAttribute"Fishing"and not aG:GetAttribute"Retractable"then
warn"[Khfresh][SelectedFish] CAST timeout -> retry same island"
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
end
return
end

if aF=="Minigame"then return end

if aF=="Cancelling"then
local aH=tick()-(tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmLastCancelAt)or tick())
if aH>=0.25 and not aG:GetAttribute"Fishing"and not aG:GetAttribute"Retractable"then
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
end
return
end
end)
task.wait(0.1)
end
end)
end,
}
end

local ao=I.AutoFarm
local ap=aj:CreateSection"Auto Farm"


local aq={}
for ar,as in ipairs(I.islands)do
table.insert(aq,as.name)

Default=ao.selectedIsland or aq[1]or"Island 1",

ao.selectedIsland=ar
end

if not ao.selectedIsland then
ao.selectedIsland=aq[1]or"Island 1"
end

Name="Enable Auto Farm Fish",
Default=ao.enabled==true,

ar=ar==true
ao.enabled=ar
if ar then

ao.inYourPosition=false
ao.inYourPositionCFrame=nil

L.Flags.MainFarm=true

M.AutoEquipRod=true
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=al.Boss==true
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=al.SecretBoss==true

enableBasicFishing(false)

L.Flags.AutoSkill=(I.savedRoutineFlags and I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoSkill)==true

Title="Auto Farm",

Duration=3

L.Flags.AnchorLock=true

resetBossFarmTarget()
restoreRoutineFlags()

end
end
}
I.autoFarmToggle=ar




local as=ap:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Farm In Your Position",
Icon="map-pin",
Default=ao.inYourPosition==true,
Callback=function(as)
as=as==true
if as then
local at=s.Character
local au=at and at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not au then
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Farm In Your Position",
Message="HumanoidRootPart not found.",
Duration=3
}
return
end

ao.enabled=true

L.Flags.MainFarm=true

L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
M.AutoEquipRod=true
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=al.Boss==true
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=al.SecretBoss==true
L.Flags.AntiAFK=al.AntiAFK==true
enableBasicFishing(false)
L.Flags.AutoSkill=(I.savedRoutineFlags and I.savedRoutineFlags.AutoSkill)==true

pcall(forceEquipRodOnce)
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Farm In Your Position",
Message="Farming at your current position — no teleport.",
Duration=3
}
else
local at=ao.inYourPositionStartedFarm==true
ao.inYourPosition=false
ao.inYourPositionCFrame=nil
ao.inYourPositionStartedFarm=false

if at then
ao.enabled=false
L.Flags.MainFarm=false
L.Flags.AutoCast=false
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
M.AutoEquipRod=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false

restoreRoutineFlags()
end

ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Farm In Your Position",
Message=at and"Disabled."or"Position mode disabled — normal Auto Farm remains active.",
Duration=2
}
end
end
}

local at
while alive()do
if ao.enabled and ao.inYourPosition then


elseif ao.enabled and ao.selectedIsland then

if aw.name==ao.selectedIsland then
au=aw
break
end
end
if au then
local av=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if av then
local aw=au.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
if at~=au.name or(av.Position-aw).Magnitude>20 then
av.CFrame=CFrame.new(aw)
end
if at~=au.name then
at=au.name
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Farm",
Message="Teleported to "..au.name,
Duration=2
}
end
end
end
else
at=nil
end
task.wait(0.5)
end
end)

Callback=function()
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then
ae:Notify{Title="Index",Message="User data not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"Index"
if not am then
ae:Notify{Title="Index",Message="Index folder not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local an=0
for ao,ap in ipairs(am:GetChildren())do
if ap:IsA"NumberValue"or ap:IsA"IntValue"then
ap.Value=1
an=an+1
elseif ap:IsA"BoolValue"then
ap.Value=true
an=an+1
end
end
ae:Notify{Title="Index",Message="Unlocked "..an.." fish!",Duration=3}
end
}
end


do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Upgrade Skill"
local function GetOwnedSkills()
local al=s and s.UserId
if not al then return{}end
local am=x and x:FindFirstChild(tostring(al))
if not am then return{}end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Skill"
if not an then return{}end
local ao={}
for ap,aq in pairs(an:GetChildren())do
if aq:IsA"Folder"then
local ar=aq:FindFirstChild"Owned"
if ar and ar:IsA"BoolValue"and ar.Value==true then
table.insert(ao,aq.Name)
end
end
end
return ao
end

local al=GetOwnedSkills()
if#al==0 then al={"No skill owned"}end
local am=al[1]or"No skill owned"

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Choose A Skill That Needs To Upgrade",
Options=al,
Default=am,
Callback=function(an)am=an end
}

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Upgrade Method",
Options={"Instant","Teleport"},
Default=I.upgradeSkill.method or"Instant",
Callback=function(an)I.upgradeSkill.method=an end
}

ak:CreateButton{
Name="Upgrade Selected Skill",
Callback=function()
if am=="No skill owned"or not am then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="No skill selected!",Duration=2}
return
end
local an=s and s.UserId
if not an then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="Player not ready!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ao=x and x:FindFirstChild(tostring(an))
if not ao then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="User data not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ap=ao:FindFirstChild"EssenceOrb"
if not ap or not ap:IsA"NumberValue"then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="EssenceOrb not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
if ap.Value<=0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="Not enough EssenceOrb!",Duration=2}
return
end
if not t or not t.UpgradeSkill then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="UpgradeSkill remote not found!",Duration=2}
return
end

I.upgradeSkill.teleported=false
I.upgradeSkill.oldPosition=nil
if I.upgradeSkill.method=="Teleport"then
local aq=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if aq then
local ar=aq:FindFirstChild"Function"
if ar then
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"Zeng Tianguo"
if as and as:IsA"Model"then
local at=as:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if at then
local au=s.Character
if au then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if av then
I.upgradeSkill.oldPosition=av.Position
av.CFrame=CFrame.new(at.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
I.upgradeSkill.teleported=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
if not I.upgradeSkill.teleported then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message=
"Could not find Zeng Tianguo, teleport failed. Continuing anyway.",Duration=3}
end
end

local aq,ar=pcall(function()
return safeInvoke("UpgradeSkill",am)
end)

if I.upgradeSkill.teleported and I.upgradeSkill.oldPosition then
task.wait(0.2)
local as=s.Character
if as then
safeRestorePosition(I.upgradeSkill)
end
end

if aq then
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="Upgraded "..am.."!",Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Upgrade Skill",Message="Failed: "..tostring(ar),Duration=2}
end
end
}
end


do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Craft Rod"
local al={
["Heavenpiercer Rod"]={
{name="Flying Fish Emperor",minKg=1000000,maxKg=3000000},

local am={"Heavenpiercer Rod","Pure Diamond Rod","Sacred Bamboo Rod"}
local an=am[1]

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select Rod to Craft",
Options=am,
Default=an,
Callback=function(ao)an=ao end
}

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Craft Method",
Options={"Instant","Teleport"},
Default="Instant",
Callback=function(ao)I.craftRodMethod=ao end
}

local function checkRodMaterials(ao)
local ap=al[ao]
if not ap then return false,"Recipe not found for "..ao end
local aq=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not aq then return false,"Player data not found"end
local ar=aq:FindFirstChild"Inventory"
if not ar then return false,"Inventory not found"end
local as={}
for at,au in ipairs(ap)do as[au.name]=0 end
for at,au in pairs(ar:GetChildren())do
local av=au.Name
if av and av~=""then
local aw
for ax,ay in ipairs(ap)do
if ay.name==av then
aw=ay;break
end
end
if aw then
local ax
if au:IsA"StringValue"then
local ay={}
for az in string.gmatch(au.Value,"[^|]+")do table.insert(ay,az)end
if#ay>=2 then ax=ay[2]:gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")end
elseif au:IsA"NumberValue"or au:IsA"IntValue"then
ax=tostring(au.Value)
end
if ax then
local ay=tonumber(ax)
if ay and ay>=aw.minKg and ay<=aw.maxKg then
as[aw.name]=as[aw.name]+1
end
end
end
end
end
local at={}
for au,av in ipairs(ap)do
local aw=as[av.name]or 0
if aw<1 then
table.insert(at,av.name.." (need 1, have "..aw..")")
end
end
if#at>0 then
return false,"Missing materials: "..table.concat(at,", ")
end
return true,"All materials ready!"
end

local function craftRod()
local ao,ap=checkRodMaterials(an)
if not ao then
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Rod",Message=ap,Duration=4}
return
end
local aq=I.craftRodMethod or"Instant"
local ar=false
if aq=="Teleport"then
local as=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if as then
local at=as:FindFirstChild"BuyFishingRod"
if at then
local au=at:FindFirstChild"Biao Di"
if au and au:IsA"Model"then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if av then
local aw=s.Character
if aw then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ax then
I.lastCraftPosition=ax.Position
ax.CFrame=CFrame.new(av.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
ar=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
local as,at=pcall(function()
safeFire("CraftRod",an)
end)
if as then
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Rod",Message="Crafted "..an.." successfully!",Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Rod",Message="Failed: "..tostring(at),Duration=2}
end
if ar and I.lastCraftPosition then
task.wait(0.3)
local au=s.Character
if au then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if av then
av.CFrame=CFrame.new(I.lastCraftPosition)
I.lastCraftPosition=nil
end
end
end
end

ak:CreateButton{Name="Craft rod now",Callback=craftRod}
ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto craft selected rod",
Default=false,
Callback=function(ao)
I.autoCraftRodRunning=ao
if ao then
if I.autoCraftRodThread then
task.cancel(I.autoCraftRodThread)
I.autoCraftRodThread=nil
end
I.autoCraftRodThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoCraftRodRunning do
pcall(craftRod)
task.wait(5)
end
end)
else
if I.autoCraftRodThread then
task.cancel(I.autoCraftRodThread)
I.autoCraftRodThread=nil
end
end
end
}
end


do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Auto Buy Bait"

local al=GetBuyableBaits()
I.selectedBait=al[1]or"No bait available"

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select Bait to Buy",
Options=al,
Default=I.selectedBait,
Callback=function(am)I.selectedBait=am end
}

ak:CreateTextbox{
Name="Buy Amount",
Placeholder="10",
Default="10",
Callback=function(am)
local an=tonumber(am)
if an and an>=1 then
I.buyBaitQuantity=clamp(math.floor(an),1,100)
end
end
}

ak:CreateTextbox{
Name="Buy When Below",
Placeholder="5",
Default="5",
Callback=function(am)
local an=tonumber(am)
if an and an>=0 then
I.buyBaitThreshold=math.floor(an)
end
end
}

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Buy Method",
Options={"Instant","Teleport"},
Default="Instant",
Callback=function(am)I.buyBaitMethod=am end
}

ak:CreateButton{
Name="Buy Now",
Callback=function()
if I.selectedBait=="No bait available"then
ae:Notify{Title="Buy Bait",Message="No bait selected",Duration=2}
return
end
local am=findBaitMerchantHRP()
local an=s.Character
local ao=an and an:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
local ap=ao and ao.CFrame


if am and ao then
ao.CFrame=CFrame.new(am.Position+Vector3.new(0,2,0))
task.wait(0.15)
end

local aq,ar=pcall(function()
safeFire("BuyBait",I.selectedBait,I.buyBaitQuantity)
end)

if ap and ao then
task.wait(0.15)
ao.CFrame=ap
end

if aq then
ae:Notify{Title="Buy Bait",Message="Bought "..
I.buyBaitQuantity.."x "..I.selectedBait,Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Buy Bait",Message="Failed: "..tostring(ar),Duration=2}
end
end
}

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Buy Bait",
Default=false,
Callback=function(am)
M.AutoBuy=am
if am then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Buy",Message="[ON] Buy "..
I.buyBaitQuantity..
" "..I.selectedBait.." when below "..I.buyBaitThreshold,Duration=3}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Buy",Message="OFF",Duration=2}
end
end
}

task.spawn(function()
local am=0
while task.wait(1)do
if not alive()then break end
if M.AutoBuy and(tick()-am)>3 then
local an=I.selectedBait
local ao=I.buyBaitQuantity
local ap=I.buyBaitThreshold
if an~="No bait available"then
local aq=-1
pcall(function()
local ar=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not ar then return end
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"Bait"
if not as then return end
local at=as:FindFirstChild(an)
if at and(at:IsA"NumberValue"or at:IsA"IntValue")then
aq=at.Value
end
end)
if aq>=0 and aq<ap then
am=tick()
local ar=findBaitMerchantHRP()
local as=s.Character
local at=as and as:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
local au=at and at.CFrame

if ar and at then
at.CFrame=CFrame.new(ar.Position+Vector3.new(0,2,0))
task.wait(0.15)
end

pcall(function()
safeFire("BuyBait",an,ao)
end)

if au and at then
task.wait(0.15)
at.CFrame=au
end

ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Buy",
Message=an..": "..aq.." left - bought "..ao,
Duration=2
}
end
end
end
end
end)
end

do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Trait Reroll"
local function GetOwnedSkillNames()
local al=x and x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then return{"No skill owned"}end
local am={}
for an,ao in pairs(al.Skill:GetChildren())do
if ao:IsA"Folder"and ao.Owned and ao.Owned.Value==true then
table.insert(am,ao.Name)
end
end
if#am==0 then return{"No skill owned"}end
return am
end

local function GetTraitNames()
local al={}
if not w or not w:FindFirstChild"Trait"then return{"No trait available"}end
for am,an in pairs(w.Trait:GetChildren())do
if an:IsA"ModuleScript"then
local ao,ap=pcall(require,an)
if ao and type(ap)=="table"and ap.Name then
table.insert(al,ap.Name)
end
end
end
if#al==0 then return{"No trait available"}end
return al
end

local al=GetOwnedSkillNames()
local am=al[1]or"No skill owned"
local an=GetTraitNames()
local ao=an[1]or"No trait available"
local ap=false

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select Skill",
Options=al,
Default=am,
Callback=function(aq)am=aq end
}

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Target Trait",
Options=an,
Default=ao,
Callback=function(aq)ao=aq end
}

ak:CreateToggle{Name="Ignore Locked",Default=false,Callback=function(
aq)ap=aq end}

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Reroll Method",
Options={"Instant","Teleport"},
Default=I.Reroll.method or"Instant",
Callback=function(aq)I.Reroll.method=aq end
}

local aq=t:FindFirstChild"RerollTrait"

ak:CreateButton{
Name="Reroll Until Trait",
Callback=function()
if I.Reroll.running then
I.Reroll.running=false
if I.Reroll.thread then
task.cancel(I.Reroll.thread)
I.Reroll.thread=nil
end
if I.Reroll.teleported and I.Reroll.oldPosition then
local ar=s.Character
if ar then
safeRestorePosition(I.Reroll)
end
end
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
return
end

local ar=am
local as=ao
if not ar or ar==""or ar=="No skill owned"then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Select a valid skill",Duration=2}
return
end
if not as or as==""or as=="No trait available"then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Select a target trait",Duration=2}
return
end
if not aq then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="RerollTrait event not found",Duration=2}
return
end
local at=x and x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
local au=at and at:FindFirstChild"Trait Reroll"
if au and au.Value<=0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="No rerolls left!",Duration=2}
return
end

I.Reroll.teleported=false
I.Reroll.oldPosition=nil
if I.Reroll.method=="Teleport"then
local av=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if av then
local aw=av:FindFirstChild"Function"
if aw then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"Nanjiang"
if ax and ax:IsA"Model"then
local ay=ax:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ay then
local az=s.Character
if az then
local aA=az:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if aA then
I.Reroll.oldPosition=aA.Position
aA.CFrame=CFrame.new(ay.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
I.Reroll.teleported=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
if not I.Reroll.teleported then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message=
"Could not find Nanjiang, teleport failed. Continuing anyway.",Duration=3}
end
end

I.Reroll.running=true
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Reroll started... Target: "..as,Duration=2}

I.Reroll.thread=task.spawn(function()
local av=0
while I.Reroll.running do
local aw=x and x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
local ax=aw and aw:FindFirstChild"Trait Reroll"
if ax and ax.Value<=0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="No rerolls left, stopping",Duration=2}
break
end
local ay,az=pcall(function()
return aq:InvokeServer(ar,ap)
end)
if not ay or not az then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Reroll failed, stopping",Duration=2}
break
end
av=av+1
local aA=az.Name
if aA==as then
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Got "..
as.." on "..ar.." after "..av.." rerolls!",Duration=3}
break
end
task.wait(0.15)
end

if I.Reroll.teleported and I.Reroll.oldPosition then
local aw=s.Character
if aw then
safeRestorePosition(I.Reroll)
end
end
I.Reroll.running=false
I.Reroll.thread=nil
ae:Notify{Title="Reroll",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
end)
end
}
end


do

else
I.craftBaitQuantity=1
end
end
}

}

local al={
["Rainbow Bait"]={
{name="Colossal Tigerfish",minKg=3000000,maxKg=6000000},
{name="Heavenpiercer Turtle",minKg=3000000,maxKg=6000000},
{name="Golden Guardian Fish",minKg=1000000,maxKg=3000000},
{name="Crimson Electric Eel",minKg=3000000,maxKg=4000000},
},
["Frost Bait"]={
{name="Frost Kingfish",minKg=4000000,maxKg=7000000},
{name="Ascended Perch",minKg=900000,maxKg=1000000},
{name="Primordial Kunfish Overlord",minKg=1000000,maxKg=1600000},
{name="Warbringer Shark",minKg=500000,maxKg=800000},
},
["Nameless Bait"]={
{name="Mountain Fish",minKg=1000000,maxKg=5000000},
{name="Tiger Mirefish",minKg=1000000,maxKg=3000000},
{name="Mirage Lanternfish",minKg=3000000,maxKg=5000000},
{name="Octoparasitic Fish",minKg=3000000,maxKg=5000000},
}
}

local function checkMaterials(am,an)
local ao=al[am]
if not ao then return false,"No recipe found for "..am end
local ap=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not ap then return false,"Player data not found"end
local aq=ap:FindFirstChild"Inventory"
if not aq then return false,"Inventory not found"end
local ar={}
for as,at in ipairs(ao)do ar[at.name]=0 end
for as,at in pairs(aq:GetChildren())do
local au=at.Name
if au and au~=""then
local av
for aw,ax in ipairs(ao)do
if ax.name==au then
av=ax;break
end
end
if av then
local aw
if at:IsA"StringValue"then
local ax={}
for ay in string.gmatch(at.Value,"[^|]+")do table.insert(ax,ay)end
if#ax>=2 then aw=ax[2]:gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")end
elseif at:IsA"NumberValue"or at:IsA"IntValue"then
aw=tostring(at.Value)
end
if aw then
local ax=tonumber(aw)
if ax and ax>=av.minKg and ax<=av.maxKg then
ar[av.name]=ar[av.name]+1
end
end
end
end
end
local as={}
for at,au in ipairs(ao)do
local av=ar[au.name]or 0
if av<an then
table.insert(as,au.name.." (can "..an..", co "..av..")")
end
end
if#as>0 then
return false,"Missing materials: "..table.concat(as,", ")
end
return true,"Materials are sufficient!"
end

local function craftBait()
local am=I.craftBaitQuantity
local an,ao=checkMaterials(I.craftBaitName,am)
if not an then
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Bait",Message=ao,Duration=4}
return
end
local ap=I.craftBaitMethod or"Instant"
local aq=false
if ap=="Teleport"then
local ar=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if ar then
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"BuyBait"
if as then
local at=as:GetChildren()
if#at>=5 then
local au=at[5]
if au and au:IsA"Model"then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if av then
local aw=s.Character
if aw then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ax then
I.lastCraftBaitPosition=ax.Position
ax.CFrame=CFrame.new(av.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
aq=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
local ar,as=pcall(function()
safeFire("CraftBait",I.craftBaitName,am)
end)
if ar then
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Bait",Message="Crafted "..
am.."x "..I.craftBaitName,Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Craft Bait",Message="Failed: "..tostring(as),Duration=2}
end
if aq and I.lastCraftBaitPosition then
task.wait(0.3)
local at=s.Character
if at then
local au=at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if au then
au.CFrame=CFrame.new(I.lastCraftBaitPosition)
I.lastCraftBaitPosition=nil
end
end
end
end

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto craft bait",
Default=false,
Callback=function(am)
I.autoCraftRunning=am
if am then
if I.autoCraftThread then
task.cancel(I.autoCraftThread)
I.autoCraftThread=nil
end
I.autoCraftThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoCraftRunning do
pcall(craftBait)
task.wait(5)
end
end)
else
if I.autoCraftThread then
task.cancel(I.autoCraftThread)
I.autoCraftThread=nil
end
end
end
}

ak:CreateButton{Name="Craft bait now",Callback=craftBait}
end


do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Auto Gacha"
local function GetTicketCount()
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then return 0 end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"Gacha"
if not am then return 0 end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Tickets"
if an and an:IsA"NumberValue"then
return an.Value
end
return 0
end

local function GetCrystals()
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then return 0 end
local am={"Crystals","Gems","Diamonds","Crystal","Gem"}
for an,ao in ipairs(am)do
local ap=al:FindFirstChild(ao)
if ap and ap:IsA"NumberValue"then
return ap.Value
end
end
local an=al:FindFirstChild"Currency"
if an then
for ao,ap in ipairs(am)do
local aq=an:FindFirstChild(ap)
if aq and aq:IsA"NumberValue"then
return aq.Value
end
end
end
return 0
end

local al={"Egoless Banner","Celestial Banner"}
local am=al[1]
local an={"1","10"}
local ao="1"

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select Banner",
Options=al,
Default=am,
Callback=function(ap)am=ap end
}
ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Tickets per roll",
Options=an,
Default=ao,
Callback=function(ap)ao=ap end
}

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Gacha",
Default=false,
Callback=function(ap)
I.autoGachaRunning=ap
if ap then
if I.autoGachaThread then
task.cancel(I.autoGachaThread)
I.autoGachaThread=nil
end
I.autoGachaThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoGachaRunning do
pcall(function()
local aq=GetTicketCount()
local ar=tonumber(ao)or 1
local as=GetCrystals()
local at=false
if aq>=ar then
at=true
else
local au=ar-aq
local av=au*5
if as>=av then
at=true
else
ae:Notify{
Title="Gacha",
Message="Not enough tickets/crystals! Need "..
av.." crystals (have "..as.."). Stopping.",
Duration=4
}
I.autoGachaRunning=false
return
end
end
if at then
local au=(ao=="10")
safeFire("Gacha",au,am)
ae:Notify{
Title="Gacha",
Message="Rolled "..ao.." ticket(s) on "..am,
Duration=2
}
end
end)
task.wait(2)
end
end)
else
if I.autoGachaThread then
task.cancel(I.autoGachaThread)
I.autoGachaThread=nil
end
ae:Notify{Title="Gacha",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
end
end
}
end
end

}

ak:CreateButton{
Name="Exchange now",
Callback=function()
local an=s.UserId
local ao=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(an))
if not ao then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Player data not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ap=ao:FindFirstChild"Crystal"
local aq=ao:FindFirstChild"Cash"
if not ap or not aq then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Crystal or Cash not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ar=al
local as=ar*5
local at=ar*5000000
if ap.Value<as then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Not enough Crystals! Need "..
as..", have "..ap.Value,Duration=3}
return
end
if aq.Value<at then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Not enough Cash! Need "..
at..", have "..aq.Value,Duration=3}
return
end

I.exchange.teleported=false
I.exchange.oldPosition=nil
if I.exchange.method=="Teleport"then
local au=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if au then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"Function"
if av then
local aw=av:FindFirstChild"Nanjiang"
if aw and aw:IsA"Model"then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"

local ay=s.Character
if ay then
local az=ay:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"

I.exchange.oldPosition=az.Position
az.CFrame=CFrame.new(ax.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
I.exchange.teleported=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
if not I.exchange.teleported then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message=
"Could not find Nanjiang, teleport failed. Continuing anyway.",Duration=3}
end
end

safeFire("Exchange",am,ar)
end)

if I.exchange.teleported and I.exchange.oldPosition then
task.wait(0.2)
local aw=s.Character
if aw then
safeRestorePosition(I.exchange)
end
end

if au then
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Exchanged "..ar.." "..am.."!",Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Exchange",Message="Exchange failed: "..tostring(av),Duration=2}
end
end
}
end


do
local aj=d:CreateTab"Quests"




do
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Daily Quests"
local al=false
local am

local function teleportToIsland(an)
if not an then return false end
local ao=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not ao then return false end
local ap=an.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
ao.CFrame=CFrame.new(ap)
return true
end

local function doDailyQuest()
local an=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not an then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="User data not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ao=an:FindFirstChild"Quest"and an.Quest:FindFirstChild"Daily"
if not ao then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Daily quest folder not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
local ap={}
for aq,ar in pairs(ao:GetChildren())do
if ar:IsA"Folder"then
table.insert(ap,ar)
end
end
if#ap==0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="No daily quests available!",Duration=2}
return
end
local aq=ap[math.random(1,#ap)]
local ar=aq.Name
local as=aq:FindFirstChild"Progression"
local at=aq:FindFirstChild"QuestType"
if not as or not at then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Missing Progression or QuestType in quest "..
ar,Duration=2}
return
end
local au=as:IsA"NumberValue"and as.Value or 0
local av=at:IsA"StringValue"and at.Value or""

ae:Notify{
Title="Daily Quest",
Message="Selected quest "..ar.." | Type: "..av.." | Progress: "..au,
Duration=4
}

if av=="PlayTime"then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="PlayTime quest: just play for 30 minutes!",Duration=3}
return
end

if av=="Fishing"then
local aw=getRecommendedIsland()
if not aw then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="No suitable island found!",Duration=2}
return
end
if not teleportToIsland(aw)then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Teleport failed!",Duration=2}
return
end
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Fishing daily started on "..aw.name,Duration=2}
while al do
local ax=aq:FindFirstChild"Progression"
if ax and ax:IsA"NumberValue"then
if ax.Value>=100 then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Fishing daily completed!",Duration=3}
break
end
end
task.wait(2)
end
elseif av=="BossQuest"then
local aw=getRecommendedIsland()
if not aw then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="No suitable island found!",Duration=2}
return
end
if not teleportToIsland(aw)then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Teleport failed!",Duration=2}
return
end
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=true
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Boss daily started on "..aw.name,Duration=2}
while al do
local ax=aq:FindFirstChild"Progression"
if ax and ax:IsA"NumberValue"then
if ax.Value>=10 then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Boss daily completed!",Duration=3}
break
end
end
task.wait(2)
end
elseif av=="Bait"then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Bait daily: buying Basic Bait...",Duration=2}
local aw=t:FindFirstChild"BuyBait"
if not aw then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="BuyBait remote not found!",Duration=2}
return
end
while al do
local ax=aq:FindFirstChild"Progression"
if ax and ax:IsA"NumberValue"then
if ax.Value>=25 then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Bait daily completed!",Duration=3}
break
end
end
pcall(function()aw:FireServer("Basic Bait",1)end)
task.wait(0.5)
end
else
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Unknown quest type: "..av,Duration=2}
end
restoreRoutineFlags()
end


ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Do Daily Quest",
Default=false,
Callback=function(an)
al=an
if an then
if am then
task.cancel(am)
am=nil
end
am=task.spawn(function()
while al do
pcall(doDailyQuest)
if al then
task.wait(5)
end
end
end)
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Auto Daily started!",Duration=2}
else
if am then
task.cancel(am)
am=nil
end
restoreRoutineFlags()
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Quest",Message="Auto Daily stopped",Duration=2}
end
end
}


ak:CreateButton{
Name="Claim Daily Rewards",
Callback=function()
local an=t:FindFirstChild"DailyReward"
if not an then
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Rewards",Message="DailyReward remote not found!",Duration=3}
return
end
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Rewards",Message="Claiming all 7 daily rewards...",Duration=2}
task.spawn(function()
for ao=1,7 do
pcall(function()
an:FireServer(ao)
end)
task.wait(0.5)
end
ae:Notify{Title="Daily Rewards",Message="All 7 daily rewards claimed!",Duration=3}
end)
end
}
end

local function matGetIslandsForFish(ap)
local aq={}
for ar,as in ipairs(an)do
local at=al[as]
if at and at[ap]then table.insert(aq,as)end
end
return aq

local function matGetMaterialCount(ap,aq,ar)
local as=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not as then return 0 end
local at=as:FindFirstChild"Inventory"
if not at then return 0 end
local au=0
for av,aw in pairs(at:GetChildren())do
if aw.Name==ap then
local ax
if aw:IsA"StringValue"then
local ay={}
for az in string.gmatch(aw.Value,"[^|]+")do table.insert(ay,az)end
if#ay>=2 then ax=ay[2]:gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")end
elseif aw:IsA"NumberValue"or aw:IsA"IntValue"then
ax=tostring(aw.Value)
end
if ax then
local ay=tonumber(ax)
if ay and ay>=aq and ay<=ar then
au=au+1
end
end
end
end
return au
end

local function matTeleportToIsland(ap)
local aq=ao[ap]
if not aq then return false end
local ar=s.Character
if not ar then return false end
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if as then
as.CFrame=CFrame.new(aq.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
return true
end
return false
end

local function matGetWeather()
local ap=s:FindFirstChild"PlayerGui"and s.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild"MainGui"
if ap then
local aq=ap:FindFirstChild"Info"
local ar=aq and aq:FindFirstChild"Info"
local as=ar and ar:FindFirstChild"Weather"
local at=as and as:FindFirstChild"Value"
if at then
local au=at:IsA"TextLabel"and at.Text or at.Value
return(au:gsub("Weather:%s*",""))
end
end
return"Clear"
end

local function matCancelFishing()
local ap=s.Character
if not ap then return end
if not ap:GetAttribute"Fishing"then return end
local aq=ap:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if aq then pcall(function()safeFire("Fishing",aq.CFrame)end)end
if L.BossFarm.CurrentToken then
pcall(function()safeFire("FishingMinigame",false,L.BossFarm.CurrentToken)end)
end
end

local function matEnableFishing()
if not I.materialFarmingSavedFlags then
I.materialFarmingSavedFlags={
AutoCast=L.Flags.AutoCast,
AnchorLock=L.Flags.AnchorLock,
AutoSkill=L.Flags.AutoSkill,
AutoSkillCheck=L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck,
AntiAFK=L.Flags.AntiAFK,
AutoFarmBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss,
AutoFarmSecretBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss,
AutoEquipRod=M.AutoEquipRod,
}
end
L.Flags.AutoCast=true
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
L.Flags.AutoSkill=true
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=true
L.Flags.AntiAFK=true
M.AutoEquipRod=true
end

local function matDisableFishing()
if I.materialFarmingSavedFlags then
L.Flags.AutoCast=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoCast
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
L.Flags.AutoSkill=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoSkill
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoSkillCheck
L.Flags.AntiAFK=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AntiAFK
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoFarmBoss
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoFarmSecretBoss
M.AutoEquipRod=I.materialFarmingSavedFlags.AutoEquipRod
I.materialFarmingSavedFlags=nil
else
L.Flags.AutoCast=false
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
L.Flags.AutoSkill=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
end
end

local function matRunCycle()
if not I.autoFishForMaterialsRunning then return end
local at=s.Character
if not at then return end

local au=ap[I.materialBaitName]or aq[I.materialBaitName]
if not au or#au==0 then return end

local av,aw=math.huge
for ax,ay in ipairs(au)do
local az=matGetMaterialCount(ay.name,ay.minKg,ay.maxKg)
if az<av then
av=az;aw=ay
end
end
if not aw then return end

local ax=aw.name
local ay=string.lower(tostring(matGetWeather()))
local az=ar[ax]

if az then
local aA=false
for aB,aC in pairs(az.weathers)do if ay==aB then
aA=true;break
end end
if not aA then
I.materialCurrentTarget=nil
ae:Notify{Title="Material Farming",Message=ax..
" can weather "..az.label..". Waiting to join the server...",Duration=3}
pcall(function()
i.TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,game.JobId,s)
end)
task.wait(3)
return
end
local aB=as[ax]
local aC=at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if aB and aC then
if(aC.Position-aB.Position).Magnitude>5 then
if at:GetAttribute"Fishing"then
matCancelFishing();task.wait(0.1)
end
aC.CFrame=aB
end
else
matTeleportToIsland(az.island)
end
matEnableFishing()
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=true
M.AutoEquipRod=true
I.materialCurrentTarget=ax
return
end

local aA=matGetIslandsForFish(ax)
if#aA==0 then return end
local aB=aA[1]
if aB=="Secret Boss"then
matEnableFishing()
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=true
M.AutoEquipRod=true
I.materialCurrentTarget=ax
return
end
local aC=ao[aB]
if not aC then return end
local aD=at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not aD then return end
if(aD.Position-aC.cframe.Position).Magnitude>0.5 then
if at:GetAttribute"Fishing"then
matCancelFishing();task.wait(0.1)
end
matTeleportToIsland(aB)
end
matEnableFishing()
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
I.materialCurrentTarget=ax
end

ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Select bait for material farming",
Options={"Rainbow Bait","Frost Bait","Nameless Bait","Heavenpiercer Rod","Pure Diamond Rod","Sacred Bamboo Rod"},
Default="Rainbow Bait",
Callback=function(at)I.materialBaitName=at end
}

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto fish for bait materials",
Default=false,
Callback=function(at)
I.autoFishForMaterialsRunning=at
if at then
if I.autoFishForMaterialsThread then
task.cancel(I.autoFishForMaterialsThread)
I.autoFishForMaterialsThread=nil
end
I.materialCurrentTarget=nil
I.autoFishForMaterialsThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoFishForMaterialsRunning do
pcall(matRunCycle)
task.wait(0.5)
end
if not I.autoFishForMaterialsRunning then matDisableFishing()end
end)
ae:Notify{Title="Material Farming",Message="Started",Duration=2}
else
if I.autoFishForMaterialsThread then
task.cancel(I.autoFishForMaterialsThread)
I.autoFishForMaterialsThread=nil
end
matDisableFishing()
I.materialCurrentTarget=nil
ae:Notify{Title="Material Farming",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
end
end
}
end

local an
local ao

local function clearIslandDropdown()
if ao and ao._frame then
pcall(function()
if type(ao.Destroy)=="function"then
ao:Destroy()
elseif ao._frame and type(ao._frame.Destroy)=="function"then
ao._frame:Destroy()
end
end)
end
ao=nil
an=nil
end


local function createIslandDropdown()
clearIslandDropdown()
an=ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Choose an island",
Options=ar,
Default=aq,
Callback=function(as)
aq=as
end
}
ao=an
end


ak:CreateDropdown{
Name="Island Selection Mode",
Options={"Auto Select Island","Choose Island Manual"},
Default="Auto Select Island",
Callback=function(as)
ap=as
if as=="Choose Island Manual"then
createIslandDropdown()
else
clearIslandDropdown()
end
end
}





local function getTargetIsland()
if ap=="Auto Select Island"then
return getRecommendedIsland()
else
for as,at in ipairs(I.islands)do
if at.name==aq then
return at
end
end
return nil
end
end


local function parseQuestString(as)
if not as or as==""then return nil,nil end
local at={}
for au in string.gmatch(as,"[^,]+")do table.insert(at,au)end
if#at>=3 then
local au
local av
if string.find(at[1],"Boss")then
au=tonumber(at[3])
av=at[1]
else
au=tonumber(at[2])
av=at[3]
end
if au and av then return au,av end
end
return nil,nil
end

local function getCurrentQuestProgress()
local as=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not as then return nil end
local at=as:FindFirstChild"Quest"
if not at then return nil end
local au=at:FindFirstChild"Main"
if not au then return nil end
for av,aw in pairs(au:GetChildren())do
if aw:IsA"Folder"and(aw.Name=="Easy Ticket Quest"or aw.Name=="Hard Ticket Quest")then
local ax
local ay
local az
local aA=aw.Name
for aB,aC in pairs(aw:GetChildren())do
if aC:IsA"NumberValue"or aC:IsA"IntValue"then
if aC.Name=="1"then
ax=aC.Value
end
end
end
local aB=aw:FindFirstChild"Objective"
if aB then
for aC,aD in pairs(aB:GetChildren())do
if aD:IsA"StringValue"and aD.Name=="1"then
local aE=aD.Value
if aE and aE~=""then
local aF,aG=parseQuestString(aE)
if aF and aG then
ay=aF
az=aG
end
end
end
end
end
if ax and ay and az then
return ax,ay,aA,az
end
end
end
return nil
end


R=function(as)
local at
pcall(function()
local au=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
local av=au and au:FindFirstChild"Function"
at=av and av:FindFirstChild"Ticket Quest Giver"
end)
if not at then return false end
local au={at}
local av=s.Character
local aw=av and av:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
local ax
if at:IsA"Model"then
ax=at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
end
local ay=aw and aw.CFrame
if aw and ax then
pcall(function()aw.CFrame=CFrame.new(ax.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))end)
task.wait(0.4)
end
pcall(function()
safeFire("ChooseDialogueOption","Ticket Quest Giver",1,as,au)
end)
task.wait(0.3)
if ay and aw and aw.Parent then
pcall(function()aw.CFrame=ay end)
end
return true
end


S=function()
return R"Quest"
end

local function getTicketQuestGiver()
local as=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
local at=as and as:FindFirstChild"Function"
local au=at and at:FindFirstChild"Ticket Quest Giver"
if not au then return nil,nil end
local av=au:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart",true)
if not av and au:IsA"BasePart"then av=au end
return au,av
end

local function teleportToTicketQuestGiver()
local as,at=getTicketQuestGiver()
if not as or not at then return false end
local au=s.Character
local av=au and au:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not av then return false end
av.CFrame=CFrame.new(at.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
return true
end

Name="Teleport to Quest Giver",
Default=I.ticketTeleportToGiver~=false,
Callback=function(as)
I.ticketTeleportToGiver=as==true
end
}

ak:CreateButton{
Name="Teleport to Ticket Quest Giver",
Callback=function()
if teleportToTicketQuestGiver()then
ae:Notify{Title="Ticket Quest",Message="Teleported to Quest Giver.",Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Ticket Quest",Message="Ticket Quest Giver not found.",Duration=3}
end
end
}

local function getAcceptAction()
return(am=="Hard Quest")and"HardAcceptQuest"or"EasyAcceptQuest"
end

local function hasAnyTicketQuest()
local as=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not as then return false end
local at=as:FindFirstChild"Quest"
if not at then return false end
local au=at:FindFirstChild"Main"
if not au then return false end
for av,aw in pairs(au:GetChildren())do
if aw:IsA"Folder"then
local ax=aw.Name
if ax=="Easy Ticket Quest"or ax=="Hard Ticket Quest"then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end


local function updateQuestStatus()
local as,at,au,av=getCurrentQuestProgress()


if not as or not at or not au then
if I.ticketQuestActive then
I.ticketQuestActive=false
I.ticketTargetCount=0
I.ticketCurrentCount=0
disableAllFishing()
end
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=false
I.ticketQuestClaimed=false


if not hasAnyTicketQuest()then
local aw=tick()
if aw-(I._lastTicketClaimAt or 0)>=2.0 then
I._lastTicketClaimAt=aw
local ax=getAcceptAction()
if R(ax)then
task.wait(0.6)
if hasAnyTicketQuest()then
ae:Notify{
Title="Ticket Quest",
Message="Auto claimed "..tostring(am),
Duration=2
}
end
end
end
end
return
end


I.ticketTargetCount=at
I.ticketCurrentCount=as
I.ticketQuestActive=true


if as>=at then
if not I.ticketQuestClaimed then
ae:Notify{Title="Quest",Message="Complete! Turning in...",Duration=2}
if R"Quest"then
local aw=false
for ax=1,10 do
task.wait(0.3)
if not getCurrentQuestProgress()then
aw=true
break
end
end
if aw then
I.ticketQuestClaimed=true
disableAllFishing()
task.wait(0.2)
local ax=getAcceptAction()
R(ax)
ae:Notify{
Title="Ticket Quest",
Message="Turned in + claimed next "..tostring(am),
Duration=2
}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Quest",Message="Turn-in failed, retrying...",Duration=2}
end
else
ae:Notify{Title="Quest",Message="Giver not found, retrying...",Duration=2}
end
end
I.ticketQuestActive=false
return
end

I.ticketQuestClaimed=false


local aw=getTargetIsland()
if aw then
local ax=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ax then
local ay=aw.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
if(ax.Position-ay).Magnitude>25 then
ax.CFrame=CFrame.new(ay)
end
end
end

if av and string.find(av:lower(),"boss")then
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=false
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=true
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
elseif av=="Fishing"then
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=false
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
pcall(function()safeFire"UpdateFishProgression"end)
elseif av=="UseBait"then
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=true
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
M.AutoBuy=true
local ax=GetBuyableBaits()
local ay=false
for az,aA in ipairs(ax)do
if aA=="Basic Bait"then
I.selectedBait=aA
ay=true
break
end
end
if not ay then
I.selectedBait=ax[1]or"No bait available"
end
M.AutoEquipBait=true
else
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=false
enableBasicFishing(true)
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=false
end
end

local function startAutoDoLoop()
if I.autoDoThread then return end
I.autoDoThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoDoRunning do
pcall(updateQuestStatus)

task.wait(0.35)
end
I.autoDoThread=nil
end)
end

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Do Ticket Quest (Fast)",
Default=false,
Callback=function(as)
I.autoDoRunning=as==true
if as then
if I.autoDoThread then
task.cancel(I.autoDoThread)
I.autoDoThread=nil
end
startAutoDoLoop()
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Do Quest",
Message="FULL mode ON — claim + do + turn-in ("..tostring(am)..")",
Duration=3
}
else
if I.autoDoThread then
task.cancel(I.autoDoThread)
I.autoDoThread=nil
end
disableAllFishing()
I.isDoingUseBaitQuest=false
I.autoDoRunning=false
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Do Quest",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
end
end
}

local as
ak:CreateToggle{

I.autoRecommendRunning=at

if av then

as=au.name
ae:Notify{
Title="Recommend",
Message="Teleported to "..
au.name.." (Power "..(au.power or 0)..")",
Duration=2
}
end
end
end)
task.wait(3)
end
end)
else
if I.autoRecommendThread then
task.cancel(I.autoRecommendThread)
I.autoRecommendThread=nil
end
as=nil
end
end
}
end

I.selectedOptions=an
else
I.selectedOptions=am
end

local function checkResources(am,an)
local ao=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not ao then return false,"Player data not found"end
local ap={
Cash={path=ao:FindFirstChild"Cash",needed=5000000000},
Level={path=ao:FindFirstChild"Level"and ao.Level:FindFirstChild"Level",needed=500},
Crystal={path=ao:FindFirstChild"Crystal",needed=1000},
Ticket={path=ao:FindFirstChild"Ticket",needed=200}
}
local function getValue(aq)
if aq and aq.path then
if aq.path:IsA"NumberValue"or aq.path:IsA"IntValue"then
return aq.path.Value
end
end
return 0
end
local aq=ap[am]
local ar=ap[an]
if not aq or not ar then return false,"Invalid option"end
local as=getValue(aq)
local at=getValue(ar)
if as>=aq.needed and at>=ar.needed then
return true,"Enough"
else
local au=am..
" ("..as.."/"..aq.needed..") or "..an.." ("..at.."/"..ar.needed..")"
return false,"Not enough: "..au
end
end

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Auto Ascend",
Default=false,
Callback=function(am)
I.autoRebirthRunning=am
if am then
if I.autoRebirthThread then
task.cancel(I.autoRebirthThread)
I.autoRebirthThread=nil
end
I.autoRebirthThread=task.spawn(function()
while I.autoRebirthRunning do
pcall(function()
if#I.selectedOptions~=2 then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message="Please select exactly 2 options!",Duration=2}
task.wait(2)
return
end
local an,ao=checkResources(I.selectedOptions[1],
I.selectedOptions[2])
if an then
I.upgradeCharacter.teleported=false
I.upgradeCharacter.oldPosition=nil
if I.upgradeCharacter.method=="Teleport"then
local ap=q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
if ap then
local aq=ap:FindFirstChild"Function"
if aq then
local ar=aq:FindFirstChild"The Shadow"
if ar and ar:IsA"Model"then
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if as then
local at=s.Character
if at then
local au=at:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if au then
I.upgradeCharacter.oldPosition=au.Position
au.CFrame=CFrame.new(as.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
I.upgradeCharacter.teleported=true
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end
end
end
end
if not I.upgradeCharacter.teleported then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message=
"Could not find The Shadow, teleport failed. Continuing anyway.",Duration=3}
end
end

local ap=I.UpgradeCharacter and I.UpgradeCharacter:InvokeServer(I.selectedOptions)
if ap and ap.ok then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message="Upgraded successfully!",Duration=2}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message="Upgrade failed: "..
(ap and ap.msg or"unknown error"),Duration=3}
end

if I.upgradeCharacter.teleported and I.upgradeCharacter.oldPosition then
local aq=s.Character
if aq then
safeRestorePosition(I.upgradeCharacter)
end
end
else
if math.random(1,5)==1 then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message=ao,Duration=3}
end
end
end)
task.wait(2)
end
end)
else
if I.autoRebirthThread then
task.cancel(I.autoRebirthThread)
I.autoRebirthThread=nil
end
if I.upgradeCharacter.teleported and I.upgradeCharacter.oldPosition then
local an=s.Character
if an then
safeRestorePosition(I.upgradeCharacter)
end
end
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Ascend",Message="Stopped",Duration=2}
end
end
}

local am=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not am then return end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Cash"and am.Cash.Value or 0
local ao=am:FindFirstChild"Level"and am.Level:FindFirstChild"Level"and
am.Level.Level.Value or 0
local ap=am:FindFirstChild"Crystal"and am.Crystal.Value or 0
local aq=am:FindFirstChild"Ticket"and am.Ticket.Value or 0
ae:Notify{
Title="Resources",
Message=string.format("Cash: %.2fB | Level: %d | Crystal: %d | Ticket: %d",an/1e9,ao,ap,
aq),
Duration=4
}
end
ak:CreateButton{Name="Check Resources",Callback=displayStats}
end

local function npcRoot()
return q:FindFirstChild"NPC"
end

local function findNpcHrp(am)
local an
pcall(function()
local ao=npcRoot()
if not ao then return end
local ap=ao:FindFirstChild"Function"
local aq=(ap and ap:FindFirstChild(am))or ao:FindFirstChild(am)
if aq then an=aq:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"or aq:FindFirstChild"Torso"end
end)
return an
end

local function teleportNearNpc(am,an)
local ao=findNpcHrp(am)
local ap=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not ao or not ap then return false end
pcall(function()
ap.CFrame=CFrame.new(ao.Position+(an or Vector3.new(0,3,0)))
end)
return true
end

local function getFishKg(am)
local an=0
pcall(function()
if am:IsA"StringValue"then
local ao={}
for ap in string.gmatch(am.Value,"[^|]+")do table.insert(ao,ap)end
if#ao>=2 then an=tonumber((ao[2]:gsub("%D","")))or 0 end
elseif am:IsA"NumberValue"or am:IsA"IntValue"then
an=tonumber(am.Value)or 0
end
end)
return an
end

local function collectBigFish(am)
local an={}
local ao=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
local ap=ao and ao:FindFirstChild"Inventory"
if not ap then return an end
for aq,ar in pairs(ap:GetChildren())do
local as=getFishKg(ar)
if as>=am then
table.insert(an,{item=ar,name=ar.Name,kg=as})
end
end
table.sort(an,function(aq,ar)return aq.kg>ar.kg end)
return an
end

local function giveFishToDumbGuy(am)
if not am or not am.item then return false end
local an
pcall(function()
local ao=npcRoot()
local ap=ao and ao:FindFirstChild"Function"
an=ap and ap:FindFirstChild"Dumb guy"
end)
local ao=s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
local ap=ao and ao.CFrame
if teleportNearNpc("Dumb guy",Vector3.new(0,3,0))then task.wait(0.4)end

pcall(function()safeFire("StartDialogue","Dumb guy",an)end)
task.wait(0.15)


local aq={
{an,am.item},
{an,am.name,am.kg},
{an,am.item.Name,am.item.Value},
{am.item},
}
local ar=false
for as,at in ipairs(aq)do
local au=pcall(function()
safeFire("ChooseDialogueOption","Dumb guy",1,"GiveFish",at)
end)
if au then ar=true end
task.wait(0.12)
end

if ap and ao and ao.Parent then
task.wait(0.3)
pcall(function()ao.CFrame=ap end)
end
return ar
end

local function getCurrentWeather()
local am=v:FindFirstChild"Info"
if am then
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Info"
if an then
local ao=an:FindFirstChild"Weather"
if ao then
local ap=ao:FindFirstChild"Value"
if ap then
local aq=ap:IsA"TextLabel"and ap.Text or ap.Value
return aq:gsub("Weather:%s*","")
end
end
end
end
return"Clear"
end

an.." for "..am,Duration=4}
end
return
end
end
else
ae:Notify{Title="Weather TP",Message="Unknown weather island mapping for: "..
am,Duration=4}
end
end
}
