do
local am=aj:CreateSection("Server","Left")
local an=i.HttpService
local ao=i.TeleportService
local ap=0
local aq


local ar=""

local function b64decode(as)
local at="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
as=tostring(as or""):gsub("[^"..at.."=]","")
return(as:gsub(".",function(au)
if au=="="then return""end
local av,aw="",at:find(au,1,true)
if not aw then return""end
aw=aw-1
for ax=6,1,-1 do
av=av..((aw%2^ax-aw%2^(ax-1)>0)and"1"or"0")
end
return av
end):gsub("%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?",function(au)
if#au~=8 then return""end
local av=0
for aw=1,8 do
if au:sub(aw,aw)=="1"then
av=av+2^(8-aw)
end
end
return string.char(av)
end))
end

am:CreateTextbox{
Name="Join by Job ID",
Placeholder="Paste Job ID here...",
Default="",
Callback=function(as)
as=tostring(as or""):gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
if as==""then return end

local at=as
local au=string.match(as,"NEXUSHUB|([^%s]+)")
if au then
local av,aw=pcall(b64decode,au)
if av and aw and aw~=""then
at=aw
end
end

if at==game.JobId then
ae:Notify{
Title="Server Hop",
Message="Already in this server.",
Duration=3
}
return
end

if F then
pcall(function()khAutoSave"join by job id"end)
else
pcall(function()khSaveConfigNow()end)
end

if I.autoExecuteOnHop then
local av,aw=khQueueAutoExecute()
if not av then
ae:Notify{
Title="Auto Execute",
Message="Cannot queue script: "..tostring(aw),
Duration=4
}
return
end
end

ar=at
aq=game.JobId
ap=tick()

ae:Notify{
Title="Server Hop",
Message="Joining Job ID...",
Duration=3
}

local av,aw=pcall(function()
ao:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
game.PlaceId,
at,
s
)
end)
if not av then
av,aw=pcall(function()
ao:Teleport(game.PlaceId,s)
end)
end

if not av then
ae:Notify{
Title="Server Hop",
Message="Teleport failed: "..tostring(aw),
Duration=4
}
end
end
}

}
end
}


local function getServers(as)
local at="https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..tostring(game.PlaceId).."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
if as and as~=""then
at=at.."&cursor="..an:UrlEncode(as)
end
local au
local av,aw=pcall(function()
if syn and syn.request then
local av=syn.request{Url=at,Method="GET"}
return av and(av.Body or av.body)
elseif http_request then
local av=http_request{Url=at,Method="GET"}
return av and(av.Body or av.body)
elseif request then
local av=request{Url=at,Method="GET"}
return av and(av.Body or av.body)
elseif fluxus and fluxus.request then
local av=fluxus.request{Url=at,Method="GET"}
return av and(av.Body or av.body)
end
return game:HttpGet(at)
end)
if av and type(aw)=="string"then au=aw end
if not au or au==""then return nil end
local ax,ay=pcall(function()return an:JSONDecode(au)end)
return ax and ay or nil
end

local function collectServers()
local as,at={},{}
local au=""
for av=1,10 do
local aw=getServers(au)
if not aw then break end
if type(aw.data)=="table"then
for ax,ay in ipairs(aw.data)do
local az=tostring(ay.id or"")
local aA=tonumber(ay.playing)or 999
local aB=tonumber(ay.maxPlayers)or 999
if az~=""and az~=game.JobId and az~=aq
and not at[az]and aB>0 and aA<aB then
at[az]=true
as[#as+1]={
id=az,
playing=aA,
maxPlayers=aB,
}
end
end
end
au=tostring(aw.nextPageCursor or"")
if au==""then break end
end
return as
end

local function chooseServer(as)
local at=collectServers()
if#at==0 then return nil end

if as=="Random Server"then
return at[math.random(1,#at)]
end


local au={}
for av,aw in ipairs(at)do
if aw.playing==1 then
au[#au+1]=aw
end
end
if#au==0 then return nil end
return au[math.random(1,#au)]
end

local function hopToServer(as,at)
if tick()-ap<3 then
ae:Notify{Title="Server Hop",Message="Please wait a moment before hopping again.",Duration=2}
return false
end

local au=chooseServer(as or"1 Player Server")
if not au then
ae:Notify{
Title="Server Hop",
Message=(as=="Random Server")and"No available public server found."or"No server with exactly 1 player found.",
Duration=4
}
return false
end

if I.autoExecuteOnHop then
local av,aw=khQueueAutoExecute()
if not av then
ae:Notify{Title="Auto Execute",Message="Cannot queue script: "..tostring(aw),Duration=4}
return false
end
end

if F then khAutoSave(at or"server hop")end
aq=game.JobId
ap=tick()
ae:Notify{
Title="Server Hop",
Message=(as or"1 Player Server").." → "..tostring(au.playing).."/"..tostring(au.maxPlayers).." joining...",
Duration=3
}

local av,aw=pcall(function()
local av=Instance.new"TeleportOptions"
av.ServerInstanceId=tostring(au.id)
ao:TeleportAsync(game.PlaceId,{s},av)
end)
if not av then

av,aw=pcall(function()
ao:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,tostring(au.id),s)
end)
end
if not av then
ae:Notify{Title="Server Hop",Message="Teleport failed: "..tostring(aw),Duration=4}
return false
end
ae:Notify{Title="Server Hop",Message="Teleport request sent → "..tostring(au.playing).."/"..tostring(au.maxPlayers),Duration=2}
return true
end

am:CreateDropdown{
Name="Server Hop Mode",
Options={"1 Player Server","Random Server"},
Default=(E.ServerHopMode=="Random Server")and"Random Server"or"1 Player Server",
Callback=function(as)
E.ServerHopMode=tostring(as or"1 Player Server")
khAutoSave"server hop mode"
end
}

am:CreateButton{
Name="Server Hop — 1 Player",
Callback=function()
hopToServer("1 Player Server","manual 1-player server hop")
end
}

am:CreateButton{
Name="Server Hop — Random",
Callback=function()
hopToServer("Random Server","manual random server hop")
end
}
end

local aq=false
local ar
local as
local at
local au={}


local function startSpectate()
if aq then return end


local av
for aw,ax in ipairs(j:GetPlayers())do
if ax~=s then
av=ax
break
end
end

if not av then
ae:Notify{Title="Spectate",Message="No other players to spectate!",Duration=2}
if I.spectateToggle then
I.spectateToggle:Set(false)
end
return
end


if not av.Character then
ae:Notify{Title="Spectate",Message=av.Name.." has no character!",Duration=2}
if I.spectateToggle then
I.spectateToggle:Set(false)
end
return
end

local aw=av.Character:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not aw then
ae:Notify{Title="Spectate",Message="Cannot find HumanoidRootPart of "..av
.Name,Duration=2}
if I.spectateToggle then
I.spectateToggle:Set(false)
end
return
end

aq=true
ar=av

local ax=workspace.CurrentCamera
as=ax.CameraSubject
at=ax.CameraType


ax.CameraSubject=aw
ax.CameraType=Enum.CameraType.Custom

ae:Notify{Title="Spectate",Message="Spectating "..av.Name,Duration=2}
end


local function stopSpectate()
aq=false
ar=nil


pcall(function()
local av=workspace.CurrentCamera
if as then
av.CameraSubject=as
as=nil
end
if at then
av.CameraType=at
at=nil
end
end)

ae:Notify{Title="Spectate",Message="Stopped spectating",Duration=2}
end


local av=am:CreateToggle{
Name="Spectate Player",
Default=false,
Callback=function(av)
if av then
startSpectate()
else
stopSpectate()
end
end
}


I.spectateToggle=av


local function onTargetCharacterAdded()
if aq and ar then
local aw=ar.Character
if aw then
local ax=aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ax then
workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject=ax
end
end
end
end


local aw
local ax=j.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(ax)
if aq and ax==ar then
stopSpectate()
if I.spectateToggle then
I.spectateToggle:Set(false)
end
end
end)


local function monitorTarget()
if aq and ar then
if aw then aw:Disconnect()end
aw=ar.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
onTargetCharacterAdded()
end)
else
if aw then
aw:Disconnect()
aw=nil
end
end
end


local ay=startSpectate
startSpectate=function()
ay()
monitorTarget()
end


local az=stopSpectate
stopSpectate=function()
az()
if aw then
aw:Disconnect()
aw=nil
end
end

table.insert(au,{conn=ax})
end

do
local aj=d:CreateTab"Settings"
local ak=aj:CreateSection"Character"

ak:CreateSlider{
Name="Walk Speed",
Range={0,300},
Default=tonumber(E.WalkSpeed)or 16,
Increment=1,
Color=Color3.fromRGB(44,153,93),
Callback=function(al)
if ae.SiriusChar then ae.SiriusChar.WalkSpeed=al end
pcall(function()s.Character.Humanoid.WalkSpeed=al end)
end
}

local function startFly()
local al=s.Character
if not al or not al:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"then return end
if I.flying then return end
if I.bg then I.bg:Destroy()end
if I.bv then I.bv:Destroy()end
local am=al.HumanoidRootPart
I.bg=Instance.new("BodyGyro",am)
I.bg.P=9e4
I.bg.maxTorque=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
I.bg.cframe=am.CFrame
I.bv=Instance.new("BodyVelocity",am)
I.bv.velocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
I.bv.maxForce=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
I.flying=true
if I.flyRenderConnection then I.flyRenderConnection:Disconnect()end
I.flyRenderConnection=l.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not I.flying or not I.bg or not I.bv then return end
local an=s.Character
local ao=an and an:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not ao then return end
local ap=r.CFrame.LookVector
local aq=an.Humanoid and an.Humanoid.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero
local ar=Vector3.new(aq.X,0,aq.Z)
local as=(I.flyKeys.Up and 1 or 0)-(I.flyKeys.Down and 1 or 0)
I.bv.Velocity=ar*I.flySpeed+Vector3.new(0,as*I.flySpeed,0)
local at=Vector3.new(ap.X,0,ap.Z)
if at.Magnitude>0.01 then
I.bg.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(ao.Position,ao.Position+at.Unit)
end
end)
local an=al:FindFirstChildOfClass"Humanoid"
if an then an.PlatformStand=true end
if I.flyInputConnection then I.flyInputConnection:Disconnect()end
if I.flyInputEndConnection then I.flyInputEndConnection:Disconnect()end
I.flyInputConnection=n.InputBegan:Connect(function(ao,ap)
if ap then return end
if ao.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Space then I.flyKeys.Up=true end
if ao.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl then I.flyKeys.Down=true end
end)
I.flyInputEndConnection=n.InputEnded:Connect(function(ao)
if ao.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Space then I.flyKeys.Up=false end
if ao.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl then I.flyKeys.Down=false end
end)
end

local function stopFly()
I.flying=false
if I.flyRenderConnection then
I.flyRenderConnection:Disconnect()
I.flyRenderConnection=nil
end
I.flyKeys.Up,I.flyKeys.Down=false,false
if I.flyInputConnection then
I.flyInputConnection:Disconnect();I.flyInputConnection=nil
end
if I.flyInputEndConnection then
I.flyInputEndConnection:Disconnect();I.flyInputEndConnection=nil
end
if I.bg then
I.bg:Destroy();I.bg=nil
end
if I.bv then
I.bv:Destroy();I.bv=nil
end
local al=s.Character
if al then
local am=al:FindFirstChildOfClass"Humanoid"
if am then am.PlatformStand=false end
end
end

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Fly Mode",
Default=false,
Callback=function(al)
M.FlyMode=al
if ae.SetFlight then
ae.SetFlight(al)
else
if al then startFly()else stopFly()end
end
end
}
ak:CreateSlider{
Name="Fly Speed",
Range={1,25},
Default=3,
Increment=1,
Color=Color3.fromRGB(170,37,46),
Callback=function(al)
if ae.SiriusChar then ae.SiriusChar.FlightSpeed=al end
I.flySpeed=al*15
end
}
ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Noclip",
Default=false,
Callback=function(al)
M.Noclip=al
if ae.SetNoclip then ae.SetNoclip(al)end
end
}
ak:CreateToggle{Name="Infinite Jump",Default=false,Callback=function(al)M.InfiniteJump=al end}

local function findWaterParts()
local al={}
for am,an in pairs(q:GetDescendants())do
if an:IsA"BasePart"then
local ao=an.Name:lower()
if ao:match"water"or ao:match"ocean"or an.Material==Enum.Material.Water then
table.insert(al,an)
end
end
end
return al
end

local function enableWaterWalk()
if I.waterWalkEnabled then return end
I.waterWalkEnabled=true
I.waterPartsList=findWaterParts()
for al,am in ipairs(I.waterPartsList)do am.CanCollide=true end
I.waterPart=Instance.new"Part"
I.waterPart.Size=Vector3.new(16,1,16)
I.waterPart.Transparency=1
I.waterPart.Anchored=true
I.waterPart.CanCollide=true
I.waterPart.Name="WaterWalkPart"
I.waterPart.Parent=q
local al=s.Character
if al and al:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"then
I.waterY=al.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y-3.2
else
I.waterY=0
end
if I.waterWalkConnection then I.waterWalkConnection:Disconnect()end
I.waterWalkConnection=l.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not I.waterWalkEnabled then return end
local am=s.Character
if not am then return end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not an then return end
if I.waterPart then
I.waterPart.Position=Vector3.new(an.Position.X,I.waterY,an.Position.Z)
end
end)
end

local function disableWaterWalk()
if not I.waterWalkEnabled then return end
I.waterWalkEnabled=false
if I.waterWalkConnection then
I.waterWalkConnection:Disconnect()
I.waterWalkConnection=nil
end
if I.waterPart then
I.waterPart:Destroy()
I.waterPart=nil
end
for al,am in ipairs(I.waterPartsList)do
if am and am.Parent then
am.CanCollide=false
end
end
I.waterPartsList={}
end

ak:CreateToggle{
Name="Walk On Water",
Default=false,
Callback=function(al)
M.WalkOnWater=al
if al then enableWaterWalk()else disableWaterWalk()end
end
}

.ColorShift_Top,o.Brightness,o.OutdoorAmbient

M.FullBright=ar
if not ar then
o.Ambient=am
o.ColorShift_Bottom=an

o.Brightness=ap
o.OutdoorAmbient=aq
end
end
}

local function ExecuteRedeemAllCodes()
task.spawn(function()
local ar=k:FindFirstChild"Data"
if not ar then
ae:Notify{Title="Codes",Message="Data folder not found.",Duration=3}
return
end

local as=ar:FindFirstChild("Code",true)
local at=t and t:FindFirstChild"RedeemCode"
if not as then
ae:Notify{Title="Codes",Message="Code folder not found.",Duration=3}
return
end
if not at or not at:IsA"RemoteEvent"then
ae:Notify{Title="Codes",Message="RedeemCode event not found.",Duration=3}
return
end

local au=0
for av,aw in ipairs(as:GetChildren())do
local ax=aw.Name
if ax and ax~=""then
au=au+1
pcall(function()at:FireServer(ax)end)
task.wait(0.25)
end
end

if au==0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Codes",Message="No codes found.",Duration=3}
else
ae:Notify{Title="Codes",Message="Redeemed "..tostring(au).." code(s).",Duration=3}
end
end)
end

al:CreateButton{
Name="Redeem All Codes",
Callback=ExecuteRedeemAllCodes
}

al:CreateButton{
Name="Performance Mod",
Callback=function()
pcall(function()settings().Rendering.QualityLevel=Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
local ar=q:FindFirstChildOfClass"Terrain"
if ar then
ar.WaterWaveSize=0
ar.WaterWaveSpeed=0
ar.WaterReflectance=0
ar.WaterTransparency=1
end
o.GlobalShadows=false
o.FogEnd=9e9
o.ShadowSoftness=0
o.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=0
o.EnvironmentSpecularScale=0
for as,at in pairs(o:GetChildren())do
if at:IsA"BlurEffect"or at:IsA"SunRaysEffect"or at:IsA"ColorCorrectionEffect"or at:IsA"BloomEffect"or at:IsA"DepthOfFieldEffect"then
at:Destroy()
end
end
for as,at in pairs(q:GetDescendants())do
if at:IsA"BasePart"then
at.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
at.Reflectance=0
at.CastShadow=false
elseif at:IsA"Decal"or at:IsA"Texture"or at:IsA"ParticleEmitter"or at:IsA"Trail"or at:IsA"Fire"or at:IsA"Smoke"or at:IsA"Sparkles"then
at:Destroy()
end
end
ae:Notify{Title="Performance",Message="Optimized Game!",Duration=3}
end
}
al:CreateToggle{
Name="Enable Notifications",
Default=true,
Callback=function(ar)
I.notificationsEnabled=ar
end
}
end

local am=aj:CreateSection"Visual Dupe"
local an,ao="None"
local function scanVisualInventory()
local ap=s:FindFirstChildOfClass"PlayerGui"
local aq,ar={},{}
if not ap then return aq end
for as,at in ipairs(ap:GetDescendants())do
if(at:IsA"TextLabel"or at:IsA"TextButton")and at.Visible then
local au=tostring(at.Text or"")
if au:match"%d+%.?%d*%s*[Kk][Gg]"and not ar[au]then ar[au]=true;table.insert(aq,au)end
end
end
table.sort(aq);return aq
end
local ap=am:CreateDropdown{Name="Visual target (KG)",Options={"None"},Default="None",Callback=function(ap)
an=tostring(ap or"None");ao=nil
local aq=s:FindFirstChildOfClass"PlayerGui";if not aq then return end
for ar,as in ipairs(aq:GetDescendants())do
if(as:IsA"TextLabel"or as:IsA"TextButton")and as.Visible and tostring(as.Text or"")==an then ao=as:FindFirstAncestorOfClass"Frame"or as.Parent;break end
end
end}
am:CreateButton{Name="Scan Visual Inventory",Callback=function()
local aq=scanVisualInventory()
if#aq==0 then
ae:Notify{Title="Visual Dupe",Message="No KG labels found in inventory UI. Open your bag first.",Duration=3}
return
end
local ar=pcall(function()
if ap.Refresh then ap:Refresh(aq,true)
elseif ap.SetValues then ap:SetValues(aq)
end
end)
ae:Notify{Title="Visual Dupe",Message=ar and("Found "..#aq.." items")or"Dropdown refresh failed",Duration=3}
end}
am:CreateButton{Name="Create 10 Visual Copies",Callback=function()
if not ao or not ao.Parent then return end
for aq=1,10 do local ar=ao:Clone();ar:SetAttribute("KhfreshVisualOnly",true);ar.Parent=ao.Parent;if ar:IsA"GuiObject"then ar.Visible=true end end
end}
am:CreateButton{Name="Clear Visual Copies",Callback=function()
local aq=s:FindFirstChildOfClass"PlayerGui";if not aq then return end
for ar,as in ipairs(aq:GetDescendants())do if as:GetAttribute"KhfreshVisualOnly"==true then pcall(function()as:Destroy()end)end end
end}

do
local al=aj:CreateSection"Staff Watch"
local am=32032540
local an=246
local ao={
["Content Creator"]=246,Contributor=
247,
["QA Tester"]=248,Moderator=
249,Contractor=
250,
["Community Manager"]=251,Lady=
254,Owner=
255,
}
local ap={
enabled=false,
connections={},
checkInterval=nil,
}

local aq=i.HttpService

local function getUserRank(ar)
local as="https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/"..am.."/users/"..ar
local at,au=pcall(function()return aq:GetAsync(as)end)
if not at then return 0 end
local av,aw=pcall(function()return aq:JSONDecode(au)end)
if not av or not aw then return 0 end
if aw.isInGroup==false then return 0 end
return aw.role and aw.role.rank or 0
end

local function isStaff(ar)
if ar.UserId==s.UserId then return false end
local as=getUserRank(ar.UserId)
return as>=an
end

local function onStaffDetected(ar)
local as="Staff"
for at,au in pairs(ao)do
if getUserRank(ar.UserId)==au then
as=at
break
end
end
ae:Notify{
Title="⚠️ Staff Detected",
Message=string.format("%s (%s) joined. Staff Watch notification only.",ar.Name,as),
Duration=5,
}
end

local function startStaffWatch()
if ap.enabled then return end
ap.enabled=true
task.spawn(function()
for ar,as in ipairs(j:GetPlayers())do
if isStaff(as)then
onStaffDetected(as)
return
end
end
end)
local ar=j.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(ar)
task.wait(1)
if isStaff(ar)then
onStaffDetected(ar)
end
end)
table.insert(ap.connections,ar)
ap.checkInterval=task.spawn(function()
while ap.enabled do
task.wait(30)
for as,at in ipairs(j:GetPlayers())do
if isStaff(at)then
onStaffDetected(at)
return
end
end
end
end)
ae:Notify{Title="Staff Watch",Message="Enabled! Watching for staff...",Duration=3}
end

local function stopStaffWatch()
ap.enabled=false
for ar,as in ipairs(ap.connections)do
pcall(function()as:Disconnect()end)
end
ap.connections={}
if ap.checkInterval then
task.cancel(ap.checkInterval)
ap.checkInterval=nil
end
ae:Notify{Title="Staff Watch",Message="Disabled!",Duration=2}
end

al:CreateToggle{Name="Enable Staff Watch",Default=false,Callback=function(ar)if ar then
startStaffWatch()else stopStaffWatch()end end}

al:CreateButton{
Name="Check Current Players",
Callback=function()
ae:Notify{Title="Staff Watch",Message="Checking...",Duration=2}
for ar,as in ipairs(j:GetPlayers())do
if isStaff(as)then
onStaffDetected(as)
return
end
end
ae:Notify{Title="Staff Watch",Message="No staff in this server!",Duration=3}
end
}
end

do
local al=aj:CreateSection"ESP"


local am=false
local an
local ao={}
local ap=game:GetService"ReplicatedStorage"

local function getPlayerStats(aq)
local ar={
Level="N/A",
Cash="N/A",
Rod="N/A",
Crystal="N/A",
EssenceOrb="N/A",
Bait="N/A",
FishCaught="N/A",
Ticket="N/A",
TraitReroll="N/A"
}
local as=ap:FindFirstChild"Data"
if not as then return ar end
local at=as:FindFirstChild(tostring(aq.UserId))
if not at then return ar end

local au=at:FindFirstChild"Level"
if au then
local av=au:FindFirstChild"Level"
if av then ar.Level=tostring(av.Value)end
end
local av=at:FindFirstChild"Cash"
if av then ar.Cash=tostring(av.Value)end
local aw=at:FindFirstChild"FishingRod"
if aw then ar.Rod=tostring(aw.Value)end
local ax=at:FindFirstChild"Crystal"
if ax then ar.Crystal=tostring(ax.Value)end
local ay=at:FindFirstChild"EssenceOrb"
if ay then ar.EssenceOrb=tostring(ay.Value)end
local az=at:FindFirstChild"EquippedBait"
if az then ar.Bait=tostring(az.Value)end
local aA=at:FindFirstChild"FishCaught"
if aA then ar.FishCaught=tostring(aA.Value)end
local aB=at:FindFirstChild"Ticket"
if aB then ar.Ticket=tostring(aB.Value)end
local aC=at:FindFirstChild"Trait Reroll"
if aC then ar.TraitReroll=tostring(aC.Value)end
return ar
end

local function createEspForPlayer(aq)
if aq==s then return end
local ar=aq.Character
if not ar then return end
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"Head"
if not as then return end
if as:FindFirstChild"EspBillboard"then return end

local at=getPlayerStats(aq)
local au=Instance.new"BillboardGui"
au.Name="EspBillboard"
au.Size=UDim2.new(0,300,0,110)
au.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,3,0)
au.AlwaysOnTop=true
au.Parent=as

local av=Instance.new"Frame"
av.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
av.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
av.BackgroundTransparency=0.6
av.BorderSizePixel=0
av.Parent=au

local aw=Instance.new"UICorner"
aw.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
aw.Parent=av

local ax=Instance.new"TextLabel"
ax.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18)
ax.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
ax.BackgroundTransparency=1
ax.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
ax.TextSize=16
ax.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
ax.Text=aq.Name
ax.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
ax.Parent=av

local ay={
string.format("Lv:%s | Cash:%s",at.Level,at.Cash),
string.format("Rod:%s",at.Rod),
string.format("Bait:%s",at.Bait),
string.format("Cry:%s | Ess:%s | Tick:%s | Reroll:%s",at.Crystal,at.EssenceOrb,at.Ticket,
at.TraitReroll),
string.format("Fish:%s",at.FishCaught)
}

local az=18
for aA,aB in ipairs(ay)do
local aC=Instance.new"TextLabel"
aC.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16)
aC.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,az)
aC.BackgroundTransparency=1
aC.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
aC.TextSize=12
aC.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200)
aC.Text=aB
aC.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
aC.Parent=av
az=az+16
end

table.insert(ao,{player=aq,billboard=au})
end

local function removeEspForPlayer(aq)
local ar=aq.Character
if not ar then return end
local as=ar:FindFirstChild"Head"
if not as then return end
local at=as:FindFirstChild"EspBillboard"
if at then at:Destroy()end
for au,av in ipairs(ao)do
if av.player==aq then
table.remove(ao,au)
break
end
end
end

local function clearAllEsp()
for aq,ar in ipairs(ao)do
pcall(function()
if ar.billboard and ar.billboard.Parent then
ar.billboard:Destroy()
end
end)
end
ao={}
end

local function updateAllEsp()
if not am then return end
clearAllEsp()
for aq,ar in ipairs(j:GetPlayers())do
if ar~=s then
pcall(function()createEspForPlayer(ar)end)
end
end
end

local function startEsp()
if am then return end
am=true
updateAllEsp()

local aq=j.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(aq)
if am then
task.wait(0.5)
pcall(function()createEspForPlayer(aq)end)
end
end)
table.insert(ao,{conn=aq})

local ar=j.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(ar)
pcall(function()removeEspForPlayer(ar)end)
end)
table.insert(ao,{conn=ar})

local as=j.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(as)
if am then
as.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
pcall(function()createEspForPlayer(as)end)
end)
end
end)
table.insert(ao,{conn=as})

an=task.spawn(function()
while am do
task.wait(10)
if am then
pcall(updateAllEsp)
end
end
end)

ae:Notify{Title="ESP",Message="Players ESP enabled!",Duration=2}
end

local function stopEsp()
am=false
if an then
task.cancel(an)
an=nil
end
clearAllEsp()
for aq,ar in ipairs(ao)do
if ar.conn then
pcall(function()ar.conn:Disconnect()end)
end
end
ao={}
ae:Notify{Title="ESP",Message="Players ESP disabled!",Duration=2}
end

al:CreateToggle{
Name="Players",
Default=false,
Callback=function(aq)
if aq then
startEsp()
else
stopEsp()
end
end
}


do
local aq=false
local ar
local as={}
local at=workspace:FindFirstChild"Boat"

local function createBoatEsp(au)
if not au or not au:IsA"Model"then return end
if as[au]then return end


if not au.Parent or not au.Parent:IsA"Folder"then return end

local av=au:FindFirstChildWhichIsA"BasePart"
if not av then
av=au:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
end
if not av then return end

local aw="Boat"
local ax=au.Parent
if ax and ax~=at then
aw=ax.Name
end

local ay=Instance.new"Highlight"
ay.FillColor=Color3.fromRGB(0,150,255)
ay.FillTransparency=0.5
ay.OutlineColor=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,255)
ay.OutlineTransparency=0.3
ay.Adornee=au
ay.Parent=au

local az=Instance.new"BillboardGui"
az.Name="BoatEspBillboard"
az.Size=UDim2.new(0,150,0,30)
az.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2,0)
az.AlwaysOnTop=true
az.Parent=av

local aA=Instance.new"Frame"
aA.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
aA.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
aA.BackgroundTransparency=0.5
aA.BorderSizePixel=0
aA.Parent=az

local aB=Instance.new"UICorner"
aB.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
aB.Parent=aA

local aC=Instance.new"TextLabel"
aC.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
aC.BackgroundTransparency=1
aC.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
aC.TextSize=14
aC.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,255)
aC.Text="Boat | "..aw
aC.TextScaled=false
aC.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
aC.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
aC.Parent=aA

as[au]={
highlight=ay,
billboard=az,
attachPart=av,
}
end

local function removeBoatEsp(au)
local av=as[au]
if av then
if av.highlight then av.highlight:Destroy()end
if av.billboard then av.billboard:Destroy()end
as[au]=nil
end
end

local function clearAllBoatEsp()
for au,av in pairs(as)do
pcall(function()
if av.highlight then av.highlight:Destroy()end
if av.billboard then av.billboard:Destroy()end
end)
end
as={}
end

local function scanAllBoats()
if not at then return end
for au,av in ipairs(at:GetDescendants())do
if av:IsA"Model"and av.Parent and av.Parent:IsA"Folder"then
if not as[av]then
pcall(function()createBoatEsp(av)end)
end
end
end
end

local function startBoatEsp()
if aq then return end
aq=true
scanAllBoats()

if at then
local au=at.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(au)
if aq and au:IsA"Model"and au.Parent and au.Parent:IsA"Folder"then
task.wait(0.1)
pcall(function()
if not as[au]then
createBoatEsp(au)
end
end)
end
end)
table.insert(ao,{conn=au})
end

ar=task.spawn(function()
while aq do
task.wait(10)
if aq then
pcall(function()
for au,av in pairs(as)do
if not au.Parent then
removeBoatEsp(au)
end
end
scanAllBoats()
end)
end
end
end)

ae:Notify{Title="Boat ESP",Message="Boat ESP enabled!",Duration=2}
end

local function stopBoatEsp()
aq=false
if ar then
task.cancel(ar)
ar=nil
end
clearAllBoatEsp()
ae:Notify{Title="Boat ESP",Message="Boat ESP disabled!",Duration=2}
end

al:CreateToggle{
Name="Boat ESP",
Default=false,
Callback=function(au)
if au then
startBoatEsp()
else
stopBoatEsp()
end
end
}
end

local ay=Instance.new"Frame"
ay.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
ay.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
ay.BackgroundTransparency=0.5
ay.BorderSizePixel=0
ay.Parent=ax

local az=Instance.new"UICorner"
az.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
az.Parent=ay

local aA=Instance.new"TextLabel"
aA.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
aA.BackgroundTransparency=1
aA.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
aA.TextSize=14
aA.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0)
aA.Text=au.Name
aA.TextScaled=false
aA.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
aA.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
aA.Parent=ay

as[au]={
highlight=aw,
billboard=ax,
attachPart=av,
}
end

local function removeSecretRodEsp(au)
local av=as[au]
if av then
if av.highlight then av.highlight:Destroy()end
if av.billboard then av.billboard:Destroy()end
as[au]=nil
end
end

local function clearAllSecretRodEsp()
for au,av in pairs(as)do
pcall(function()
if av.highlight then av.highlight:Destroy()end
if av.billboard then av.billboard:Destroy()end
end)
end
as={}
end

local function scanAllSecretRods()
if not at then return end
for au,av in ipairs(at:GetDescendants())do
if av:IsA"Model"and av.Parent and av.Parent:IsA"Folder"then
if not as[av]then
pcall(function()createSecretRodEsp(av)end)
end
end
end
end

local function startSecretRodEsp()
if aq then return end
aq=true
scanAllSecretRods()

if at then
local au=at.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(au)
if aq and au:IsA"Model"and au.Parent and au.Parent:IsA"Folder"then
task.wait(0.1)
pcall(function()
if not as[au]then
createSecretRodEsp(au)
end
end)
end
end)
table.insert(ao,{conn=au})
end

ar=task.spawn(function()
while aq do
task.wait(10)
if aq then
pcall(function()
for au,av in pairs(as)do
if not au.Parent then
removeSecretRodEsp(au)
end
end
scanAllSecretRods()
end)
end
end
end)

ae:Notify{Title="Secret Rod ESP",Message="Secret Rod ESP enabled!",Duration=2}
end

local function stopSecretRodEsp()
aq=false
if ar then
task.cancel(ar)
ar=nil
end
clearAllSecretRodEsp()
ae:Notify{Title="Secret Rod ESP",Message="Secret Rod ESP disabled!",Duration=2}
end

al:CreateToggle{
Name="Secret Rod ESP",
Default=false,
Callback=function(au)
if au then
startSecretRodEsp()
else
stopSecretRodEsp()
end
end
}
end

N=function()
if not alive()then return false end

if a.AntiAFKConnection then
pcall(function()a.AntiAFKConnection:Disconnect()end)
a.AntiAFKConnection=nil
end
if a.AntiAFKThread then
pcall(function()task.cancel(a.AntiAFKThread)end)
a.AntiAFKThread=nil
end

local aj=m
if not aj then
local ak,al=pcall(function()return game:GetService"VirtualUser"end)
if ak then aj=al end
end
if not aj or not s then
a.AntiAFKInitialized=false
return false
end

a.AntiAFKInitialized=true
a.AntiAFKConnection=s.Idled:Connect(function()
if not alive()or L.Flags.AntiAFK==false then return end
pcall(function()
aj:CaptureController()
aj:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0,0))
end)
pcall(function()
local ak=t and t:FindFirstChild"AFK"
if ak then ak:FireServer"Stay Active"end
end)
end)

a.AntiAFKThread=task.spawn(function()
while alive()do
task.wait(45)
if not alive()then break end
if L.Flags.AntiAFK~=false then
pcall(function()
aj:CaptureController()
aj:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(20,20))
end)
end
end
a.AntiAFKThread=nil
end)
return true
end

O=function()
if a.AntiAFKConnection then
pcall(function()a.AntiAFKConnection:Disconnect()end)
a.AntiAFKConnection=nil
end
if a.AntiAFKThread then
pcall(function()task.cancel(a.AntiAFKThread)end)
a.AntiAFKThread=nil
end
a.AntiAFKInitialized=false
end


do
local aj
local ak
l.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not alive()then return end
if not M.Noclip then return end
local al=s.Character
if not al then return end
if ak~=al then
ak=al
aj={}
for am,an in ipairs(al:GetDescendants())do
if an:IsA"BasePart"then aj[#aj+1]=an end
end
end
for am,an in ipairs(aj)do
if an.Parent and an.CanCollide then an.CanCollide=false end
end
end)

n.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
if M.InfiniteJump and s.Character then
local al=s.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass"Humanoid"
if al then al:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)end
end
end)

end

I.AutoSaveConfig=false
E.AutoSaveConfig=false





















local aj=i.GuiService
local ak=s.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild"KhfreshPanel"

local function DisableKhfreshKeyboardActivation(al)
if al:IsA"GuiObject"then
al.Selectable=false
end
end

if a.KhfreshSpaceGuardConnection then
a.KhfreshSpaceGuardConnection:Disconnect()
a.KhfreshSpaceGuardConnection=nil
end
if a.KhfreshSelectableGuardConnection then
a.KhfreshSelectableGuardConnection:Disconnect()
a.KhfreshSelectableGuardConnection=nil
end

if ak then
DisableKhfreshKeyboardActivation(ak)
for al,am in ipairs(ak:GetDescendants())do
DisableKhfreshKeyboardActivation(am)
end
a.KhfreshSelectableGuardConnection=ak.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(al)
task.defer(DisableKhfreshKeyboardActivation,al)
end)
end

a.KhfreshSpaceGuardConnection=n.InputBegan:Connect(function(al)
if al.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Space then
local am=aj.SelectedObject
if am and ak and am:IsDescendantOf(ak)then
aj.SelectedObject=nil
end
end
end)

local function castRod()
if not(bossFarmEnabled()or(I.AutoFarm and I.AutoFarm.enabled))then return false end
local al=s.Character
if not al then return false end
if al:GetAttribute"Fishing"and not al:GetAttribute"Retractable"then return false end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not am then return false end
L.BossFarm.LastCast=tick()
resetBossFarmTarget()
pcall(function()
safeFire("Fishing",am.CFrame)
end)
return true
end

local function farmHookedBoss(al)
if not bossFarmEnabled()then return end
local am,an=shouldFarmBossTarget(al)
if not am then return end
L.BossFarm.CurrentType=an
local ao=an..tostring(al.Name)
if L.BossFarm.LastNotify~=ao then
L.BossFarm.LastNotify=ao
ae:Notify{Title="Boss Farm",Message="Farming "..
an..": "..tostring(al.Name or"Unknown"),Duration=3}
end
L.BossFarm.LastProgress=tick()
pcall(function()safeFire"UpdateFishProgression"end)
end



do


local function pressSkillKey(al)
local am=Enum.KeyCode[al]
if not am then return false end
local an=false

pcall(function()
local ao=game:GetService"VirtualInputManager"
ao:SendKeyEvent(true,am,false,game)
ao:SendKeyEvent(false,am,false,game)
an=true
end)

pcall(function()
if type(keypress)=="function"and type(keyrelease)=="function"then
keypress(am)
keyrelease(am)
an=true
end
end)

pcall(function()
if t then
local ao=t:FindFirstChild"UseSkill"or t:FindFirstChild"Skill"
if ao and ao:IsA"RemoteEvent"then
ao:FireServer(al)
an=true
elseif ao and ao:IsA"RemoteFunction"then
ao:InvokeServer(al)
an=true
end
end
end)
return an
end















a.KhfreshFishingHeartbeat=l.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not alive()then return end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning==true then return end

local al=L.Flags.AutoFarmSkill==true
local am=L.Flags.AutoCast==true
local an=L.Flags.MainFarm==true

if not(an or am or al or L.Flags.AntiAFK)then return end

local ao=s.Character
if not ao then return end

if am then
pcall(function()



if not ao:GetAttribute"Fishing"then
local ap=t and t:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
if ap then
ap:FireServer()
end
elseif I.isDoingUseBaitQuest and v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"and v.Fishing.Visible then
local ap=t and t:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
if ap then
ap:FireServer()
end
end
end)
end


if not al then
I.skillSeqIndex=1
I.skillCycleDone=false
end

if L.Flags.AntiAFK then
pcall(function()safeFire"AFK"end)
end
end)


task.spawn(function()
local al={"Z","X","C","V"}
while alive()do
if L.Flags.AutoFarmSkill==true or L.Flags.AutoSkill==true then
local am=L.Flags.SelectedSkills
if type(am)~="table"then
am={Z=true,X=true,C=true,V=true}
end

local an=false
for ao,ap in ipairs(al)do
if am[ap]then an=true break end
end
if not an then
am={Z=true,X=true,C=true,V=true}
L.Flags.SelectedSkills=am
end

for ao,ap in ipairs(al)do
if am[ap]then
pressSkillKey(ap)
task.wait(0.05)
end
end
task.wait(0.05)
else
task.wait(0.3)
end
end
end)
end


task.spawn(function()
local al={thunderstorm=
"Island 2",rainy=
"Island 3",windy=
"Island 5",snowy=
"Island 6",frost=
"Island 6",foggy=
"Island 7",
["blazing sun"]="Island 8",
}

local function getActiveWeather()
local am=v:FindFirstChild"Info"
if am then
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Info"
if an then
local ao=an:FindFirstChild"Weather"
if ao then
local ap=ao:FindFirstChild"Value"
if ap then
local aq=ap:IsA"TextLabel"and ap.Text or ap.Value
return aq:gsub("Weather:%s*",""):lower():gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
end
end
end
end
return"clear"
end

while true do
task.wait(1.5)
if not alive()then break end
if I.SmartWeather and I.SmartWeather.enabled then
pcall(function()
local am=getActiveWeather()
local an=al[am]

if an and(am~="clear"and am~="")and I.SmartWeather.selectedIslands[an]then
if not I.SmartWeather.activeOverride then
local ao=s.Character
local ap=ao and ao:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ap then
I.SmartWeather.savedCFrame=ap.CFrame
I.SmartWeather.savedFlags={
AutoCast=L.Flags.AutoCast,
AutoFarmBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss,
AutoFarmSecretBoss=L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss,
AnchorLock=L.Flags.AnchorLock,
AutoSkill=L.Flags.AutoSkill,
AutoSkillCheck=L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck,
AntiAFK=L.Flags.AntiAFK,
}
I.SmartWeather.activeOverride=true
I.SmartWeather.targetIsland=an

for aq,ar in ipairs(I.islands)do
if ar.name:lower()==an:lower()then
ap.CFrame=CFrame.new(ar.cframe.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
break
end
end

L.Flags.AutoCast=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=false
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=true
L.Flags.AnchorLock=true
L.Flags.AutoSkill=true
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=true
L.Flags.AntiAFK=true

ae:Notify{
Title="Smart Secret Weather",
Message="Weather detected: "..
am:upper().."! Auto-hopping to "..an.." cau Secret.",
Duration=4
}
end
end
else
if I.SmartWeather.activeOverride then
ae:Notify{
Title="Smart Secret Weather",
Message="Weather ended! Returning to the original farm...",
Duration=3
}
local ao=s.Character
local ap=ao and ao:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if I.SmartWeather.savedCFrame and ap then
ap.CFrame=I.SmartWeather.savedCFrame
end
if I.SmartWeather.savedFlags then
local aq=I.SmartWeather.savedFlags
L.Flags.AutoCast=aq.AutoCast
L.Flags.AutoFarmBoss=aq.AutoFarmBoss
L.Flags.AutoFarmSecretBoss=aq.AutoFarmSecretBoss
L.Flags.AnchorLock=aq.AnchorLock
L.Flags.AutoSkill=aq.AutoSkill
L.Flags.AutoSkillCheck=aq.AutoSkillCheck
L.Flags.AntiAFK=aq.AntiAFK
end
I.SmartWeather.activeOverride=false
I.SmartWeather.savedCFrame=nil
I.SmartWeather.savedFlags=nil
I.SmartWeather.targetIsland=nil
end
end
end)
end
end
end)

task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(0.2)do
if not alive()then break end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning~=true and bossFarmEnabled()then
local al=s.Character
local am=v and v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
if al and al:GetAttribute"Fishing"then
if L.BossFarm.PendingCancel then
cancelOrReleaseNormalFish()
elseif L.BossFarm.HasHooked and am and am.Visible then
local an=getCurrentHookTarget()
local ao=shouldFarmBossTarget(an)
if ao then
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=false
farmHookedBoss(an)
elseif an.Name and an.Name~="???"then
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=true
cancelOrReleaseNormalFish()
end
end
else
resetBossFarmTarget()
castRod()
end
end
end
end)


do
local function getInventoryCount()
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then return 0 end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"Inventory"
if not am then return 0 end
local an=0
for ao in pairs(am:GetChildren())do an=an+1 end
return an
end

local function getInventoryLimit()
local al=x:FindFirstChild(tostring(s.UserId))
if not al then return 0 end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"InventoryLimit"
if am and am:IsA"NumberValue"then return am.Value end
return 0
end

local function getSellSofa()
local al=q:FindFirstChild"Map"
if al then
local am=al:FindFirstChild"Map 1"
if am then
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Build"
if an then
local ao=an:FindFirstChild"SellFish"
if ao then
return ao:FindFirstChild"Sofa"
end
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function getSofaPosition()
local al=getSellSofa()
if al then
local am=al:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if am then return am.Position end
local an=al.PrimaryPart
if an then return an.Position end
for ao,ap in ipairs(al:GetDescendants())do
if ap:IsA"BasePart"then
return ap.Position
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function teleportToSofa()
local al=getSofaPosition()
if not al then return false end
local am=s.Character
if not am then return false end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not an then return false end
I.lastPosition=an.Position
an.CFrame=CFrame.new(al+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
return true
end

local function teleportBack()
if not I.lastPosition then return end
local al=s.Character
if not al then return end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if not am then return end
am.CFrame=CFrame.new(I.lastPosition)
I.lastPosition=nil
end

local function doSell()
if not M.AutoSell then return end
if tick()-(I.sellCooldown or 0)<0.5 then return end
I.sellCooldown=tick()
local al=I.sellMethod1 or"Spam"
local am=I.sellMethod2 or"Instant"
if al=="When full"then
local an=getInventoryCount()
local ao=getInventoryLimit()
if ao==0 then return end
if an<ao-3 then return end
end
local an=false
if am=="Teleport"then
an=teleportToSofa()
if an then task.wait(0.3)end
end
pcall(function()safeFire("SellFish","All")end)
if an then
task.wait(0.2)
teleportBack()
end
end

task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(1)do
if not alive()then break end
pcall(doSell)
end
end)
end

local function selectedFarmCurrentIslandFromState()
local al=I.SelectedAutoFarmIslands or{}
if#al==0 then return nil end
local am=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex)or 1
if am<1 or am>#al then am=1 end
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex=am
return al[am]
end

local function selectedFarmIslandCompleteFromState(al)
local am=(I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland or{})[al]or{}
local an=(I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland or{})[al]or{}
local ao,ap=0,0
for aq in pairs(am)do
ao+=1
if an[aq]then ap+=1 end
end
return ao>0 and ap>=ao,ap,ao
end

local function selectedFarmAdvanceIslandFromState()
local al=I.SelectedAutoFarmIslands or{}
if#al<=1 then return end
local am=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex)or 1
am+=1
if am>#al then
am=1
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland={}
for an in pairs(I.SelectedAutoFarmFishByIsland or{})do
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland[an]={}
end
end
I.SelectedAutoFarmIslandIndex=am
end

t.FishingMinigame.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(al,am,an)


if not al or not am then return end

if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning==true then
if I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase~="WaitingForMinigame"then return end

local ao=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)or 0
if ao<=0 or ao~=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmActiveCastSerial)then return end

I.SelectedAutoFarmCastToken=an
L.BossFarm.CurrentToken=an
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Minigame"

task.spawn(function()


local ap=normalizeSelectedFishName(I.SelectedAutoFarmFishNameAtCast or"")
local aq=""
local ar=tick()
local as=ar+2.5
while tick()<as and I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning==true do
if ao~=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)then return end
aq=readProgressFishName()
if not isSelectedFishPlaceholder(aq)then



if ap==""or aq~=ap or tick()-ar>=0.45 then
break
end
end
task.wait(0.05)
end

if ao~=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)then return end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning~=true then return end
if isSelectedFishPlaceholder(aq)then
warn"[Khfresh][SelectedFish] ProgressionBar.FishName unavailable; NO CANCEL"
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Minigame"
return
end

local at=normalizeSelectedFishName(aq)
local au=I.SelectedAutoFarmFishSet or{}
local av=false
for aw,ax in pairs(au)do
if ax==true and normalizeSelectedFishName(aw)==at then
av=true
break
end
end

warn("[Khfresh][SelectedFish] ProgressionBar.FishName=["..tostring(aq).."] -> "..(av and"KEEP"or"CANCEL"))

if not av then

I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Cancelling"
I.SelectedAutoFarmLastCancelAt=tick()
local aw=s.Character
local ax=aw and aw:FindFirstChild"HumanoidRootPart"
if ax then
pcall(function()safeFire("Fishing",ax.CFrame)end)
end
return
end


local aw=tick()+45
while ao==tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)
and I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning==true do
local ax=s.Character
local ay=v and v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
local az=ax and ax:GetAttribute"Fishing"and ay and ay.Visible
if not az then break end
pcall(function()safeFire"UpdateFishProgression"end)
if tick()>=aw then
warn"[Khfresh][SelectedFish] Progress timeout; STAY on current island"
return
end
task.wait(0.08)
end

if ao~=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCastSerial)then return end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning~=true then return end

local ax=tonumber(I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland)or selectedFarmCurrentIslandFromState()
if not ax then
I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
return
end

I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland=I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland or{}
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland[ax]=I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland[ax]or{}
I.SelectedAutoFarmCaughtByIsland[ax][at]=true

local ay,az,aA=selectedFarmIslandCompleteFromState(ax)
if ay then
warn("[Khfresh][SelectedFish] Island "..tostring(ax).." COMPLETE "..tostring(az).."/"..tostring(aA))
selectedFarmAdvanceIslandFromState()
I.SelectedAutoFarmCurrentIsland=selectedFarmCurrentIslandFromState()
selectedLastIsland=nil
else
warn("[Khfresh][SelectedFish] Island "..tostring(ax).." progress "..tostring(az).."/"..tostring(aA).." — STAY")
end

I.SelectedAutoFarmPhase="Idle"
end)
return
end


L.BossFarm.CurrentTarget=al
L.BossFarm.CurrentToken=an
L.BossFarm.HasHooked=true
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=false
L.BossFarm.CurrentRunId=L.BossFarm.CurrentRunId+1
local ao=L.BossFarm.CurrentRunId
local ap=getCurrentHookTarget()
local aq=shouldFarmBossTarget(ap)
if bossFarmEnabled()and not aq then
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=true
task.delay(0.15,cancelOrReleaseNormalFish)
return
end
if L.Flags.AutoCast or(bossFarmEnabled()and aq)then
task.spawn(function()
local ar=true
local as
as=v.Fishing:GetPropertyChangedSignal"Visible":Connect(function()
if not v.Fishing.Visible then
ar=false
if as then as:Disconnect()end
end
end)
while ar do
if not alive()then break end
if bossFarmEnabled()then
if ao~=L.BossFarm.CurrentRunId then break end
ap=getCurrentHookTarget()
aq=shouldFarmBossTarget(ap)
if not aq then
L.BossFarm.PendingCancel=true
cancelOrReleaseNormalFish()
break
end
farmHookedBoss(ap)
task.wait(0.08)
else
pcall(function()safeFire"UpdateFishProgression"end)
task.wait(0.2)
end
end
if bossFarmEnabled()and ao==L.BossFarm.CurrentRunId then
resetBossFarmTarget()
end
end)
end
end)

t.Slam.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(al)
if L.Flags.MainFarm or(I.AutoFarm and I.AutoFarm.enabled)then
task.spawn(function()

pcall(function()al:FireServer"Perfect"end)
local am=v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"and v.Fishing:FindFirstChild"TrashCan"
if am and am:FindFirstChild"Slam"then pcall(function()am.Slam:Destroy()end)end
end)
end
end)




tick()




















































































l.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not alive()then return end
if I.SelectedAutoFarmFishRunning==true then return end
local al=v and v:FindFirstChild"Fishing"
if not al or not al.Visible then return end
local am=al:FindFirstChild"BarFrame"
if not am or not am:IsA"GuiObject"then return end
if am.Size.X.Scale~=1 then
am.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,am.Size.Y.Scale,am.Size.Y.Offset)
end
local an=am:FindFirstChild"Bar"
if an then
an.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
end
end)






if M.FullBright and not I.fullBrightApplied then
pcall(function()
o.Ambient=Color3.new(1,1,1)
o.ColorShift_Bottom=Color3.new(1,1,1)
o.ColorShift_Top=Color3.new(1,1,1)
o.Brightness=2
o.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.new(1,1,1)
I.fullBrightApplied=true
end)
elseif not M.FullBright and I.fullBrightApplied then
I.fullBrightApplied=false
end
return true
end
